**Test count:** **946 passing** (2026-08-11: unit 57 · integration 882 across 36 suites · isolation 7). *Earlier figure of 502 (2026-07-31) kept below for the historical breakdown.* *Down from 474: the recruitment and teacher-application suites were deleted with the feature.* *Historic note — the line below describes the M7-era count of 206 and is kept for the breakdown of what M7 added:* **206 passing** — unit 36 · integration 163 · isolation 7 (+ **12 Playwright e2e** across the 6 tenant screens, the CSV-import flow, + the /admin vendor console). `pnpm test` green (3 projects serial, matching CI). *(M7 hardening added: health-probe e2e (4), sms-monthly-credit (1), CSV-import e2e (3), phone-OTP e2e (4), + matrix-conformance rows (+15 across import & OTP); the fee-season load driver `scripts/load-fees.mjs` runs outside jest.)*
> [!note] Fixed a test-infra issue while adding rate limiting: the aggregate **`pnpm test`** ran the integration project **in parallel**, so the SMS e2e suites contended over the shared BullMQ `sms` queue (a job drained/dispatched by the wrong suite → duplicate `SmsLog`). Changed `package.json` `test` to chain `test:unit → test:integration → test:isolation` (integration/isolation `--runInBand`), matching CI exactly. Also note: a **stray `worker` process** (from `pnpm start:worker:dev` whose children survived the parent kill) will consume the queue and cause the same duplicate-dispatch — always confirm no `dist/apps/worker/main` is running before an integration run. Not a product bug (prod jobs have deterministic ids + a single worker fleet).

## 🔧 M7 / hardening progress (in flight)
- [x] **Object storage + real PDFs** (§22.6, §11/§15): `StorageService` (AWS SDK v3 — MinIO dev, R2 prod) + `PdfService` (pdfkit). Report cards & certificates now render **real PDFs → uploaded → served via 10-min presigned GET**. e2e verifies `%PDF` bytes over MinIO.
- [x] **Upload pipeline** (§22.6): presigned PUT → magic-byte/MIME allowlist validate → **ClamAV scan** → move quarantine→permanent.
- [x] **ClamAV scan wiring** (§22.6): `ClamAvService` speaks clamd **INSTREAM** over TCP (no new dep). `confirmUpload` fetches the object once, magic-byte-validates, then scans (when `CLAMAV_ENABLED=true`); infected → object deleted + **422 `FILE_INFECTED`**; scanner outage → **fail-closed 503 `VIRUS_SCAN_UNAVAILABLE`**. Opt-in (off in dev/test). **Verified against real clamd**: EICAR → FOUND, clean → OK, dead port → 503. Pure `parseResponse` + config covered by unit spec.
- [x] **Backups + PITR** (§33, §29 RPO ≤30m): nightly logical `scripts/backup-postgres.sh` (pg_dump→R2) + `scripts/restore-verify.sh` (R2 restore smoke) + **`scripts/restore-verify-local.sh`** (dev/CI: dump→scratch-restore→compare row counts). **PITR:** WAL archiving enabled in `docker-compose.prod.yml` postgres (`wal_level=replica`, `archive_mode=on`, `archive_command`, **`archive_timeout=300`** → ≤5-min RPO) + **`scripts/basebackup.sh`** (pg_basebackup→R2) with a point-in-time restore runbook. **Both halves proven locally**: `restore-verify-local.sh` round-tripped the seeded DB (8 tables incl. fee_payments match exactly); a standalone archive-config run produced WAL segments (`pg_stat_archiver`: archived 3, **failed 0**) — surfacing the real gotcha that the archive volume must be writable by the postgres uid. *(Wire both scripts to Coolify cron on deploy; prod ships WAL via wal-g/pgBackRest.)*
- [x] **Rate limiting** (§29): Redis **sliding-window** limiter (atomic Lua over a sorted set) + `RateLimitGuard` (global, runs right after `JwtAuthGuard` so authed routes key per-user, `@Public` per-IP). Named policies: login 5/IP/15min **and** 10/email/1h, refresh 60/token/1h, parent-read 600/user/1h, public 60/IP/min, authenticated default 600/user/min. `@RateLimit(name)` / `@SkipRateLimit()` decorators; **429 + `Retry-After`** via the §25.1 envelope; tenant-scoped breaches logged as a compromise signal. Verified live (6th login → 429). Gated by `RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED` (on by default; **off in the test env** so shared-Redis suites stay deterministic). Covered by service + guard unit specs.
- [x] **Next.js frontend scaffold** (`apps/web`, Next 14 App Router): login + dashboard wired to the API (cookie auth + CSRF via `lib/api.ts`); dev proxy preserves the tenant host. Typechecks + `next build` green. Separate install (`cd apps/web && pnpm install`).
- [x] **Frontend: role-based screens** — central nav/page gating (`lib/roles.ts`, `(app)/layout.tsx`) covers Dashboard/Setup/Students/Admissions/Attendance/Fees/Exams/Reports per role. **Admissions** (`app/(app)/admissions/page.tsx`): inquiry list+filter, new-inquiry form, per-row schedule/record-entry-test/admit/reject/withdraw following the inquiry state machine. **Exams** (`app/(app)/exams/page.tsx`): subjects, grade scale editor, terms, exam CRUD, open-marks-entry, bulk marks entry (section roster × class subjects), results view, publish (completeness gate), report-card generate/list. `lib/api.ts` gained `apiPatch` + `Inquiry/EntryTest/Subject/Term/GradeBand/Exam/ExamResult/ReportCard` types.
- [x] **Playwright E2E** (`playwright.config.ts`, `test/e2e/`) — **all 6 tenant screens + the vendor console covered**: `smoke` (login flow, role nav, students table), `admissions` (inquiry → entry test → admit → shows in Students), `exams` (class/section/student → subject+term+exam → open marks entry → enter marks → results → publish → report cards), `attendance` (mark LATE on a safe date, persisted on reload), `fees` (generate invoice batch → partial + full payment → PAID), `reports` (class-strength table + CSV export), and **`admin`** (platform admin → provision a throwaway tenant → list → suspend → reactivate). **11 tests green across 3 consecutive full runs** (`npx playwright test`, ~23–28s). Specs assert the POST payload at the **network layer** where a value is easy to drop (exam marks, attendance status, payment amount, provisioned subdomain) — this caught + now guards a real marks-entry state-clobber race (marks posted as 0). **Shared auth via two setup projects + `storageState`** (`auth.setup.ts` for the tenant owner, `platform-auth.setup.ts` for the platform admin; feature specs reuse the tenant session, `admin.spec.ts` overrides to the platform one): the whole suite makes ~3 form logins total so it stays under the §29 login limiter (5/IP/15min) — the earlier per-test login design blew the limit at 8 logins/run. See [[Key Decisions]].
- [x] **Campus scoping — deny-by-default** (§22.8, security playbook **P1.7**): closed a privilege gap where non-OWNER_ADMIN roles were only role-gated, not campus-scoped (a campus-A admin could list/read/act on campus-B data via a client-supplied `?campusId=B`). New shared helper `libs/common/src/authz/campus-scope.ts` (`restrictedCampusId` → `null` for OWNER_ADMIN else the user's `campusId`, **fail-closed to the nil-UUID `NO_CAMPUS` when a non-owner has no campusId**; `effectiveCampusFilter`, `assertCampusAccess`). Enforced **in services** (§22.8 — guards run before the `withTenant` tx). **Done:** Students (`search` force-scope, `getOne` 403 → also gates update/delete/all guardian ops, `createStudentCore` campus check → covers direct add **and** admissions admit), Attendance (`markBulk`/`patch` 403, `query` force-scope), Fees (`invoicing.list` force-scope, `get` 403, `createBatch` 403; `payments.pay` 403, `listPayments` force-scope). **Now also rolled out (2nd pass):** Admissions (`createInquiry`/`list` scoped, `getOne` 403 → gates schedule/record/reject/withdraw/admit), Exams (`getExam` 403 → gates open-marks/enterMarks/getResults/publish; `createExam` + `listExams` scoped), Reports (6 of 7 force-scoped via the enrollment/class campus join; `smsUsage` left school-wide — no campus dimension), HR staff (`createStaff`/`getStaff`/`listStaff`/salary/teacher-assignments scoped), Setup (`createClass`/`createSection`/`createSubject`/`updateCampus` 403, `listClasses`/`listSections`/`listSubjects` force-scoped). **Still deferred (owner-only or no campus dimension):** payroll-runs (OWNER_ADMIN-only), `payments.deposit` (parent-level advance), academic-years/terms/grade-scales (school-wide). OWNER_ADMIN unchanged (no regression). Covered by `test/integration/campus-scope.e2e-spec.ts` (2 campuses; CAMPUS_ADMIN + ACCOUNTANT bound to A) — **10 cases green** (students/attendance/fees + inquiry/exam/class+section/staff cross-campus denials); full `pnpm test` green. See [[Key Decisions]].
- [x] **Matrix-conformance harness** (§23, playbook **P1.14**): `test/matrix/permission-matrix.ts` encodes the role×endpoint policy as data; `test/integration/matrix-conformance.e2e-spec.ts` seeds one user per role (OWNER_ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN/ACCOUNTANT/TEACHER/PARENT) and drives every row against live routes — a non-admitted role **must** 403, an admitted role must not role-403. **59 cases green.** Building it **surfaced 4 real under-gating gaps, now fixed:** `GET /students` directory (was any-authenticated → PII leak), `/reports/*`, the fees read endpoints (`GET /fees/invoices|invoices/:id|payments|advances|defaulters`), and `GET /dashboard` — all now `@Roles`-gated to the intended consumers. A future `@Roles` change that diverges from the matrix fails this suite (wire it as the CI `matrix` job — currently runs inside `test:integration`). See [[Key Decisions]].
- [x] **Teacher / parent / self ownership** (§22.8, **P1.7** — the non-campus half): **teacher** scoping was already enforced in-service (attendance `assertCanMark` requires a `TeacherAssignment` for the section; exams `validateRow` requires the assigned subject) and **self** too (`payslips/mine`). Added the **guardian-of-student** checks that were missing: `libs/common/src/authz/ownership.ts` (`isAdminRole`); `report-cards.listByStudent` now 403s a non-admin who isn't a guardian of the student (was readable by any authenticated user — a real per-student leak); `leaves.createStudentLeave` requires a PARENT be the child's guardian, and `leaves.listStudentLeaves` force-scopes a PARENT to their own children. Covered by `test/integration/ownership.e2e-spec.ts` (a seeded PARENT linked to student1) — 4 cases green (own child 200 / other 403 for report-cards + leave-create; list scoped; OWNER_ADMIN unrestricted).
- [x] **Sentry error monitoring** (§31): `@sentry/node` initialised at both api + worker bootstrap; `AllExceptionsFilter` reports unhandled **500s** with tenant/request tags (`schoolId`/`userId`/`requestId` + method/url, never PII); worker captures **exhausted** SMS jobs (final attempt only). Opt-in — **no-op unless `SENTRY_DSN` is set** (dev/test/CI untouched). `flushSentry()` on worker shutdown. Covered by a mocked-SDK unit spec.
- [x] **Worker cron — cross-tenant nightly jobs** (§27): the fee jobs (`markOverdue`, `checkIntegrity`) existed only as manual admin triggers; now scheduled fleet-wide. `MaintenanceService` (`apps/worker/maintenance`) iterates **every active tenant** and runs each job in its own CLS + `withTenant` tx (a failing tenant is logged/Sentry-captured, doesn't abort the rest; suspended tenants skipped). `MaintenanceProcessor` registers BullMQ **repeatable** schedules on boot (idempotent) — `mark-overdue` 01:00, `fee-integrity-check` 01:30, **`idempotency-purge` hourly, `sms-log-purge` 02:00** (cron in server TZ → set `TZ=Asia/Karachi` in prod) — and consumes the `maintenance` queue. Fee jobs run **per active tenant** (own CLS + `withTenant`); the two **purges run globally** in one `deleteMany` on the BYPASSRLS connection (idempotency keys >48h, SMS logs >180d). `FeeJobsService` is provided directly in the worker (only global deps). fee-integrity mismatch → warn + Sentry (§27 "pages on-call"). Worker boots clean. Covered by `test/integration/maintenance.e2e-spec.ts` (fee jobs across 2 active + 1 suspended tenant; purges delete old / keep recent). *(`sms-monthly-credit` later added — see below; `payroll-run`/`report-cards-generate` are deliberately human-triggered, `attendance-archive` deferred.)*
- [x] **Observability — structured logs + metrics** (§31): **Pino** wired as the app logger in api + worker (`nestjs-pino` `LoggerModule` + `pinoConfig` in `libs/common/observability/logger.config.ts`, `app.useLogger` in both bootstraps). Every log line is JSON carrying **`requestId` (the CLS id, same as the §25.1 envelope) + `schoolId` + `userId`**; **PII redacted** (`authorization`/`cookie`/`set-cookie` headers + `password`/`cnic`/`phone`/`bankAccount`/`ownerPassword` body paths → `[redacted]`; bodies aren't logged by default anyway). **Prometheus** via `prom-client`: `MetricsService` (isolated registry + default node metrics + `http_request_duration_seconds` histogram labelled `method`/`route`/`status`), recorded by `MetricsMiddleware` on `res 'finish'` (so guard-403s/404s count too); scraped at **`GET /api/v1/metrics`** (`@Public` + `@SkipRateLimit` + host-exempt). **Verified live under Playwright-driven traffic**: JSON logs with requestId/schoolId/userId, cookie → `[redacted]`, and the metric captured every route/status (GET /students 200, POST /students 201, /dashboard, /fees/invoices, …). *(Remaining observability: OpenTelemetry tracing + guard-denial/rate-limit counters — deferred.)*
- [x] **Payslip PDF** (§13, §15): `PayrollService.payslipPdf()` renders via `PdfService.payslip`, uploads to `payslips/{sid}/{id}.pdf`, returns a 10-min presigned GET. `GET /payslips/:id/pdf` — **owner-or-admin check in the service** (§22.8, reads inside the RLS tx, not a guard). Verified e2e: real `%PDF` bytes fetched over MinIO.
- [x] **Deploy image + PITR backups** (§33): multi-stage `Dockerfile` (node:22-slim, one image → api+worker via two entrypoints) + `docker-compose.prod.yml` (self-contained pg/redis/minio, one-shot `migrate` runs `prisma migrate deploy` + SQL companions + RLS-coverage gate before apps boot). **WAL archiving** on (`wal_level=replica`, `archive_mode=on`, `archive_timeout=300` → ≤5-min RPO) into a `walarchive` volume. `scripts/basebackup.sh` (pg_basebackup → R2 + PITR runbook) and `scripts/restore-verify-local.sh` (dump→scratch-restore→row-count compare — *"a backup you've never restored isn't a backup"*). Verified: restore-verify round-trips 8 tables exactly; standalone WAL test archived 3/failed 0 (surfaced the archive-dir-must-be-writable-by-postgres-uid gotcha). api/worker containers healthy (health 200, login 200).
- [x] **Fee-season load + concurrency test** (§25.5, 2026-07-13): `scripts/load-fees.mjs` — zero-dep Node driver (150 concurrent payments on distinct invoices + 40-way same-key idempotency replay + 30-way overpay race + integrity check). **ALL INVARIANTS HELD, zero 5xx**: receipts unique + gap-free, charged exactly once, never over-collected, ledger clean. Caught + fixed two contention bugs (too-tight Prisma tx budget → aborts; idempotency reserve poisoning its own tx via P2002) — see [[Key Decisions]] → *Concurrency / load hardening* and [[Fees & Payments]].
- [x] **Web frontend container + edge ingress** (§33, 2026-07-14): `apps/web/Dockerfile` (Next 14 `output: 'standalone'` → lean runner) + `.dockerignore`; wired into `docker-compose.prod.yml` as a `web` service behind a new **Traefik** edge that path-routes one origin (`/api`→api, `/`→web) **preserving Host** so tenant subdomains resolve, and the browser calls `/api/v1/*` same-origin. Verified: image builds + serves (`/login` 200, right `<title>`); path-split + Host-preservation proven with a `traefik/whoami` file-provider test. (Traefik's docker-provider socket fails on local Docker Desktop/Windows — standard on the Linux VPS; details in [[Deployment & Operations]].)
- [x] **DR runbook + PITR drill** (§33, 2026-07-14): [[DR Runbook]] — step-by-step recovery for 4 scenarios (A logical restore, B point-in-time, C whole-VPS rebuild, D single-tenant) grounded in the real scripts, plus a post-recovery validation checklist and quarterly-drill procedure with a drill log. **Runbook B rehearsed locally end-to-end**: inserted a "good" row → base backup → recorded target time → inserted a "bad" row → restored base + replayed WAL to the target → confirmed the restored DB has only the good row (bad correctly not replayed). Gotchas captured (archive-dir uid perms; `pg_basebackup -Xstream` flakiness → use `-Xfetch`).
- [x] **Durable WAL→R2 archiving** (§29/§33, 2026-07-14): custom `docker/postgres` image (postgres:16-alpine + rclone + `scripts/archive-wal.sh`) ships each WAL segment off-box to `s3://$R2_BUCKET/wal/` (opt-in `WAL_R2_ENABLED=true`), returning non-zero until the upload lands so Postgres never recycles un-shipped WAL — so base **and** WAL live in R2 and PITR (Runbook B) survives host loss, not just logical disasters. Verified end-to-end vs S3-compatible storage (segments present locally + in the bucket, `failed_count=0`; a forced failure kept segments un-recycled). Image owns `/wal-archive` as `postgres` → retires the archive-dir uid gotcha. Details in [[Key Decisions]] + [[DR Runbook]].
- [x] **Deep readiness probe** (§19/§31, 2026-07-14): `GET /health/ready` now checks the **hard dependencies** (Postgres `SELECT 1` + Redis `PING`, each timeout-bounded at 2s) and returns **503** when any is down — previously it returned 200 with a `degraded` body, so the compose/Traefik healthcheck (`statusCode===200`) never actually failed and could route to a half-up node. Body pinpoints the failed dep (`{status,checks:{db,redis}}`); S3 deliberately **not** gated (soft dep). `/health/live` stays dependency-free. Verified live (paused Redis → 503 in ~2.1s, recovered to 200) + 4-case integration test (`test/integration/health.e2e-spec.ts`). See [[Key Decisions]].
- [x] **`sms-monthly-credit` cron wired** (§14/§27, 2026-07-14): the last cross-tenant scheduled job — on the 1st of each month, `MaintenanceService` grants every ACTIVE tenant its plan SMS credit (`PLAN_MONTHLY_SMS_CREDITS[planTier]` = BASIC 1000 / PLUS 5000 / PRO 20000), idempotent per calendar month (skips a tenant already granted this month, so signup grants aren't doubled), on the BYPASSRLS platform connection. Extracted the plan-credit map to a shared `sms-plan-credits.ts` (provisioning + worker share one source). Covered by `maintenance.e2e-spec.ts` (grants once at the right tier, idempotent on re-run). **Reviewed the rest and deliberately did NOT auto-cron them:** `payroll-run` and `report-cards-generate` are human-in-the-loop workflows (auto-running would be a domain bug); `attendance-archive` has no method and isn't GA-critical. Rationale in [[Deployment & Operations]] §Jobs.
- [x] **Fee advance auto-application** (§12): a newly-generated invoice consumes the primary guardian's standing credit — `PaymentsService.applyAdvanceToInvoice` records a `FeePayment` (method **`ADVANCE`**, new enum value) + a negative `GuardianCredit`; deposit stays a pure credit record. Covered by `fees.e2e`.
- [x] **Student self-service portal** (§28, matrix §23 STUDENT column): `StudentPortalService`/`Controller` (`/portal/*`, `@Roles('STUDENT')`) — read-only, self-scoped via `Student.userId` (no id from the client → cross-student access is structurally impossible). Endpoints: overview (profile + class/section + guardians + attendance% + outstanding fees), attendance, results (report cards), fees. Clean UI under `apps/web/app/(app)/me/*` with role-aware landing (`landingPath`). Covered by `student-portal.e2e` (3) + matrix row + a Playwright spec. `scripts/seed-test-users.ts` adds one login per role (incl. a linked STUDENT).
- [x] **Auth cookie fix:** session cookies are **host-only** when `COOKIE_DOMAIN` is a single-label domain (`localhost`) — browsers reject `Domain=localhost` for a `*.localhost` host, which silently dropped the cookie and bounced login. Real apex keeps its Domain (cross-subdomain). See [[Key Decisions]].
- [x] **Users & roles** (§23, §22.8): `UsersModule` — the owner provisions staff/admin logins (`POST /users`: email + role + campus + initial password → argon2-hashed, ACTIVE immediately so they can sign in). Campus-bound roles **require a campus** (OWNER_ADMIN is school-wide); a **CAMPUS_ADMIN may only grant lower roles** (ACCOUNTANT/TEACHER/STAFF) and is **forced to their own campus** (even if they name another). `GET /users` (scoped), owner-only `PATCH /users/:id` + `POST /users/:id/reset-password`. UI: a **Campuses** screen (replaces a flat user list) — each campus shows its users **grouped by role** (Campus Admins/Accountants/Teachers/Staff) with an **inline "+ Add user"** (campus pre-selected) and a per-campus **login link** (`/login?campus=<name>` — brands the shared login page; auth is school-wide, scoping is post-login), plus a **School-wide** section for owners. **Campus names are unique per school** (case-insensitive; clean 409). Verified: `users.e2e` (create → the new account actually signs in → scoping/escalation blocked → reset works), matrix rows, and live Playwright (`campuses.spec` — add campus admin + duplicate-name rejection). *(Closes the "who creates a campus admin's login" gap; invite-link flow can layer on later.)*
- [x] **Fix:** the student-portal commit left a whole-repo `tsc` break (a matrix row typed `allow: ['STUDENT']`, not a seeded role) — corrected to `allow: []` (STUDENT-only ⇒ all seeded roles denied), so CI typecheck is green again.
- [ ] Production Coolify (VPS) deploy, pen test, staging DR drill on real R2, pilot

## 2026-08-04 — authz gap, day-close, the browser suite, and the guardian link
- [x] **F8 closed — four fee READS shipped with no `@Roles`.** Filed as two; the controller had four,
  and `GET /discounts?studentId=` returned a named child's hardship/staff/sibling concessions to any
  authenticated session. Filed Low-Med, closed **High**. The matrix had rows for the *writes* only,
  which is exactly what hid it. Four rows added (24 assertions, both directions).
- [x] **G2 closed — the day close is per school.** `staffAttendance.closeAtTime` (default 20:00, so
  no behaviour changed on upgrade) + an hourly tick that settles only schools whose own local time
  has passed. Also fixed the two things it exposed: a cron-written ABSENT that *accused* the teacher
  it was about, rendered as a green `badge ok`; and a deadline nobody could see until they missed it.
- [x] **The browser suite went from 9 red / 29 to 27 passed · 2 skipped · 0 failed**, then 28 with the
  guardian spec. The suite now owns an `E2E Automation` campus with its own admission officer, so it
  never takes a seat a real person holds. It surfaced a genuine bug: **CSV import was unusable by
  anybody** — officer-only on the API, on a screen the officer's nav could not open.
- [x] **B4 shipped — the guardian upload link.** Signed stateless token → public page at
  `/p/[token]` → PENDING claim. Details and the reasoning in [[Fee Submission Plan]] §5.2a.
- [x] **`drainSms` consolidated.** Three hand-copied helpers meant three copies of the same two bugs,
  fixed one at a time over separate sessions. Now `test/integration/support/sms.ts`.

- [x] **B5 shipped — receipts.** A real `feeReceipt` PDF, the office gets it offered the moment a
  payment is collected, and a family sees their own on `/me/fees`. Rendered on demand (a receipt
  is a *view*, and the invoice around it moves), and refused outright for a reversed payment.
- [x] **B6 shipped — as a seam, deliberately.** `psid` column, `ONLINE` method, HMAC webhook that
  verifies, resolves the tenant, guards replays — and then returns **501**, because writing the
  payment needs a service account that does not exist yet. Details in [[Fee Submission Plan]] §5.3a.

- [x] **G3 closed — the class register has a deadline that surfaces rather than polices.**
  `attendanceMarkByTime`, an admin-only "which registers are unmarked today" query that starts
  from the SECTIONS, and a dashboard chip that appears only after the deadline and names the
  sections. Nothing blocks, nothing is auto-marked: an unmarked day stays an honest gap.
  It also caught a live lie — `todayAttendancePercent` read **100% with 9 of 17 students marked**
  on the operator's own tenant. Coverage now sits beside the percentage.

**Test count: 694 integration (32 suites) · 52 unit · 7 isolation · 28 Playwright (2 skipped).**

Open next: **G4** (per-tenant timezone — latent until a tenant sits outside Pakistan, and now
load-bearing in two places), **G5** (staff backfill floor — a decision to record either way),
G6–G8 and G10 (low). The aggregator seam waits on a merchant agreement plus the service-account
decision.

## ✅ M1 — Foundations (Done)
Blueprint §16–§22, §34. See [[Multi-Tenancy & Isolation]], [[Security & Compliance]].

## ✅ M4 — Fees end-to-end (Done)
Blueprint §12. See [[Fees & Payments]]. Gate met: collect-fee E2E green + `fee-integrity-check` clean throughout.
- [x] Fee heads / structures / late-fee policy CRUD
- [x] **Idempotent invoice batches** (`[schoolId, classId, month, year]`; duplicate → existing, `generated:0`) — synchronous generation in the request tx *(async batching = later scale optimisation)*
- [x] Discounts (PERCENT then FIXED, capped at base) applied as negative line items; **fines** via `mark-overdue` (FLAT/PER_DAY, single upserted FINE line, total recomputed)
- [x] **Payments:** reusable **IdempotencyService** (reserve-then-run, replay, 409 on hash mismatch) + `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` row lock + **gap-free per-school receiptNo** + partial→paid status; overpayment → `OVERPAYMENT_USE_ADVANCE`
- [x] **Reversals** (OWNER_ADMIN, `RV-` receipt, invoice recomputed), **waivers** (WAIVER line zeroes remaining), advances (GuardianCredit deposit + balance), defaulters
- [x] **Receipt SMS** (FEE_RECEIPT) via the BullMQ pipeline (critical send, overdraft-eligible)
- [x] `fee-integrity-check` (totalAmount = Σ items; paidAmount = Σ payments − Σ reversals)
- **Tests:** fees e2e (batch → idempotent payment replay → overpay guard → PAID → integrity → receipt SMS → reversal → mark-overdue+fine → waiver → advance) green.
- **Findings:** in a **nested** `items.create` under an invoice, do NOT pass `schoolId` — it's part of the invoice's composite relation FK and Prisma derives it (passing it → `Unknown argument schoolId`). See [[Key Decisions]]. Money uses JS-number arithmetic rounded to 2dp; switch to decimal.js for production rigor (noted).
- **M4 deferred:** advance **auto-application** to new invoices (oldest-first), sibling-discount auto-calc, reconciliation CSV import, wiring `mark-overdue`/`fee-integrity-check` to the worker cron (currently service methods + admin triggers).

## ➕ Access management — role toggles + fine-grained module access (2026-07-21)
Reworked how the owner grants specialist access so it never creates a new login, and made each capability's functionality individually controllable.
- [x] **Consolidated role-grant into one endpoint:** removed `PATCH /users/:id/hr-access` + `/campus-admin`; replaced with **`PATCH /users/:id/access { role, grant }`** (`SetAccessDto`, `ACCESS_GRANTABLE_ROLES` = HR_MANAGER/CAMPUS_ADMIN/ACCOUNTANT/ADMISSION_CONTROLLER). Single `UsersService.setAccess` over the existing `grantRole` (audit action mapped per role; generic `ROLE_ACCESS_GRANTED/REVOKED` added). Reuses the employee's existing account — **no new credentials**. `hr-access.e2e` + matrix + frontend `api.users.setHrAccess/setCampusAdmin` re-pointed to `/access`.
- [x] **One-campus-admin-per-campus rule:** `assertNoOtherCampusAdmin` blocks a 2nd CAMPUS_ADMIN on a campus (409) across create/update/grant. Covered by `users.e2e`.
- [x] **Module-access system (§23 extension):** new `module_access` tenant table (migration `20260721130000`, RLS auto-covered) + a code **module catalog** (`libs/common/authz/modules.ts`: 7 modules across recruitment/admissions/fees). A granted role unlocks all its modules by default; the owner switches one off with an `allowed=false` row. **`AccessService.assert(moduleKey)`** enforces **in-service** (RLS-safe, owner bypass) — wired into recruitment (vacancies), teacher-applications (applications, hire), admissions (inquiries, admit), fees (invoicing, payments). Owner endpoints `GET/PATCH /users/:id/modules`. `/auth/me` now returns the caller's enabled `modules[]` for UI shaping. Covered by `module-access.e2e` + 3 matrix rows.
- [x] **Frontend:** `me.modules` + `hasModule()` helper; **Staff → Manage access** drawer (owner-only) — capability toggles with **nested per-module On/Off** toggles (`api.users.modules/setModule`); the old recruitment "Manage access" is superseded. **Action gating** (hide, don't disable) on recruitment (post-vacancy / shortlist-reject / hire), admissions (inquiry actions / admit), fees (generate / collect) via `hasModule`. Backend 403 remains the real guard.
- **Gates:** api+worker build ✅ · root lint ✅ · web tsc + next lint ✅ · targeted e2e **191 pass** (module-access + hr-access + users + matrix-conformance). Live-verified: `/auth/me` returns modules; owner login → all 7 keys. **Prisma-on-Windows note:** `prisma generate` fails with EPERM while `start:api:dev`/`start:worker:dev` hold the engine DLL — stop them first, generate, restart.
- [x] **Owner dashboard redesign (frontend-only, live-tested):** `/dashboard` rebuilt from a flat 6-tile grid into a **categorized, interactive** home — greeting + date header; a **"Needs attention" strip** (derived alert chips: defaulters, pending leaves, failed SMS, ready-to-admit, tests-today, new applications — each a click-through link, green "All clear" when empty); sections **Academics & Enrollment / Finance / Communication** (the §-role-shaped `visible` metrics, each tile now a `<Link>` with hover lift + "View →" affordance, amber `metric-alert` accent when actionable) plus **Admissions pipeline** and **People & Recruitment** sections fed by the existing `/inquiries/summary` + `/vacancies/summary` (each fetch fails silently → sections auto-hide for roles the API denies, so campus-admin/accountant get a correctly slimmer page). New CSS: `.section-title`, `a.metric-link` hover, `.metric-alert`. **Live-verified in the browser as owner:** real alert surfaced ("1 new teacher application →") and both click-throughs land right (chip → /recruitment showing that applicant; Active-students tile → /students). Web tsc + lint green.
- [x] **MFA: closed the enrolment gap — and a latent lockout (2026-07-23):** login returns `mfaEnrollmentRequired` for `MANDATORY_MFA_ROLES` (OWNER_ADMIN/ACCOUNTANT) but there was **no enrolment UI**, so MFA could never be switched on. Investigating showed the backend is **complete and correct** (`/auth/mfa/setup|verify|challenge`, `DELETE /auth/mfa`) — the whole gap was frontend, and worse than "missing feature": the login page did `await api.login()` then immediately `api.me()`, but an MFA account's login returns `{ mfaRequired, mfaToken }` and **sets no session cookie** — so `/auth/me` 401'd and surfaced as *"Login failed"*. **Anyone who enabled MFA would have been permanently locked out**; it stayed latent only because nobody could enrol. Fix: (1) **login page** handles `mfaRequired` with a second step that posts to `/auth/mfa/challenge` (typed via `LoginResult` + `isMfaRequired` guard, so the two shapes can't be confused again); (2) new self-service **`/security`** screen (hidden NAV entry with *no* `roles` ⇒ every authenticated user, reached from a top-bar 🔒 link) to enrol — shows the setup key for manual entry plus the full otpauth link — and to turn MFA off (password + code); (3) `/auth/me` now returns **`mfaEnabled`**, letting the app shell show a persistent banner to mandatory-MFA roles who haven't enrolled; (4) `apiDelete` gained a body arg (the disable endpoint needs one). **Live-verified the whole lifecycle** against the running API with real TOTP codes: setup 201 → verify 204 → login returns `mfaRequired` with **no session cookie** → `/auth/me` **401 (the old lockout, reproduced)** → challenge 200 → `/auth/me` 200 → disable 204 → plain login 200. Gates: api+web tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · web lint ✅ · `auth.e2e` 6/6 ✅. *(Still open from §22.5: recovery codes — `mfaVerify` has a TODO for 10 single-use codes, which needs a `recovery_codes` table; without them a lost authenticator means an admin must clear `mfa_enabled`/`mfa_secret_enc` in the DB.)*
- [x] **Multi-role brand/landing consistency (frontend-only, 2026-07-22):** found live-testing an HR Manager (a `[TEACHER, HR_MANAGER]` multi-hat): the sidebar brand read "Teacher" while the user landed on `/recruitment` (HR). Root cause — `roles.ts` had **two separately-ordered lists** (`PANEL_LABELS` vs `LANDING`) that ranked roles differently, so they disagreed for *any* multi-role user (also latent for ACCOUNTANT+ADMISSION_CONTROLLER). Replaced both with **one canonical `ROLE_INFO` priority list** (label + landing per role); `panelLabel` and `landingPath` both derive from it via a shared `primaryRole()`, so divergence is now structurally impossible. **Verified by executing the real module:** all 10 single-role label+landing pairs unchanged (no regression); TEACHER+HR_MANAGER → HR Manager/`/recruitment` (was Teacher/recruitment), ACCOUNTANT+ADMISSION_CONTROLLER → Accountant/`/dashboard` (was Admission Portal/dashboard). Live: HR-manager brand now "🏫 HR Manager", owner still "School Admin". web tsc ✅ · next lint ✅.
- [x] **Campus-Admin UX pass (frontend-only, live-tested, 2026-07-22):** four non-security findings from the CAMPUS_ADMIN actor test, each fixed + live-verified against owner (no regression). **#2a dead-end tile:** dashboard showed a Collections tile → `/fees` (Fees is OWNER/ACCOUNTANT-only) that dead-ended on "Not authorized" — added a reusable `canReach(roles, href)` in `roles.ts` (same `navItemFor`+`hasAnyRole` rule the layout gates pages with) and the dashboard now filters both tiles and attention chips by it (future-proof for any inaccessible destination). **#2b Setup owner-only writes:** the Academic-Years card showed Add-year + Set-current (both `@Roles('OWNER_ADMIN')`) → gated behind `isOwner`, read-only list kept, non-owner sees an explanatory note. **#3 campus-dropdown leak:** Students / Admissions-new-inquiry / Setup(class filter+add) offered all campuses to a campus-bound admin → applied the `myCampuses = isOwner ? campuses : campuses.filter(c=>c.id===me.campusId)` pattern (Staff/Teachers/Recruitment already did). **#4 stale copy:** Staff hint pointed at owner-only Campus Hub (now `isOwner`-only) and Recruitment's empty state pointed at the Step-4-removed "Teachers screen" (now "Staff screen"). Gates: web tsc ✅ · next lint ✅. All backend authz was already correct — these are display-only fixes.
- [x] **Security fix — cross-campus report-card leak (P1.7, 2026-07-22):** found live-testing the CAMPUS_ADMIN actor: `GET /students/:id/report-cards` returned **200 for a student in another campus**. Root cause — `ReportCardsService.listByStudent` gated only with `isAdminRole` (OWNER **or** CAMPUS_ADMIN) to skip the guardian check, but **never applied the separate campus check** (the `isAdminRole` doc even says "campus scope applied separately" — it was implemented for the guardian dimension only). A campus admin could thus read any student's academic records school-wide. Fixed by mirroring `StudentsService.getOne`'s canon: a campus-restricted admin (`restrictedCampusId`) must have the student ACTIVE-enrolled in their campus, else 403; OWNER_ADMIN stays school-wide; a non-admin (PARENT) still hits the guardian check. `listByStudent` is the **only** caller, so this is the single fix point. Regression test in `campus-scope.e2e` (own-campus student 200 / other campus 403). **Live-verified**: girls-campus admin → own student **200**, Main-campus student **403** (was 200); owner → any campus **200** (no regression); non-admin → other student **403** (guardian check intact). Gates: api tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · campus-scope + ownership **21 pass** · exams + matrix **187 pass**.
- [x] **Exams: guarded term delete (2026-07-22):** the demo Exams screen had **23 terms** (all `ExamTerm<timestamp>` e2e debris) rendered as one unreadable ` · `-joined line, and the exams module had **no `@Delete` endpoint at all** — nothing there could ever be removed. Deletion is not naively safe: **19 of the 23 terms carry report cards**, so a plain delete would hit an FK or destroy published results. Added **`DELETE /terms/:id`** (OWNER_ADMIN) with a **referential guard** — 409 `CONFLICT` naming the blockers (*"…still has 1 report card — it cannot be deleted"*) when exams/report cards reference the term, otherwise delete + `TERM_DELETED` audit. UI: the Terms card is now a scrollable table (name · year · date range · Delete) with a count badge and a confirm dialog, replacing the joined string. Covered by `exams.e2e` ("refuses to delete a term that carries academic records, but removes an unused one" — asserts the 409 + that the term survives, then creates and deletes a clean one) + a matrix row. **Live-verified**: blocked term → 409 with the message above; fresh term → 200 and gone. Gates: api tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · api+worker build ✅ · `exams.e2e` + matrix-conformance **187 pass** ✅.
  > [!note] **Jest hook-timeout gotcha:** `jest.config.js` sets no `testTimeout`, so the 5s default applies to `beforeAll` — which boots a full Nest app **and** provisions a school. With `start:api:dev` (watch), the worker and the web dev server all competing for CPU, that hook exceeds 5s and *every* test in the suite fails with "Exceeded timeout of 5000 ms for a hook" (looks catastrophic — 182 failures — but is pure contention). Stop the dev servers before an integration run, or raise `testTimeout`.
- [x] **Billing-integrity fix — a removed student kept getting invoiced (2026-07-22):** found while live-testing the new Fee-setup UI: a generated batch billed 6 students in a class that only had 2 real ones. Root cause — **`StudentsService.softDelete` set `deletedAt` but never closed the enrollment**, and every downstream read treats an ACTIVE enrollment as a seated student. DB confirmed: 10 students / 4 soft-deleted / 10 ACTIVE enrolments, **4 of them belonging to deleted students**. Three-part fix: (1) **root cause** — `softDelete` now also sets those enrolments to `WITHDRAWN` + `endedAt`; (2) **defensive** — `InvoicingService.createBatch` filters `student: { deletedAt: null }` so a removed student can never be billed even from legacy bad rows; (3) **consistency** — the dashboard `enrollmentCount` applies the same filter (it was reporting 10 while the Students directory listed 6). Regression test in `fees.e2e` ("a removed student is withdrawn from their class and never billed again": delete → asserts no ACTIVE enrolment remains → next batch generates 1, none for the removed student). **Live-verified on demo:** dashboard head-count 10 → **6**; a fresh month-8 batch for 9th generated **2 instead of 6**. Gates: api tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · `fees.e2e` 9/9 ✅ · students-import + student-registration + student-portal + matrix-conformance **184 pass** ✅. *(Related open item: `mfaEnrollmentRequired: true` is returned by login but no MFA-enrolment UI exists.)*
- [x] **Accountant + Admission-Controller frontend completion:** `Reports` nav now includes ACCOUNTANT (backend already admitted it); the Reports screen is **role-shaped** — a non-admin accountant sees only the financial reports (daily-collection, defaulters, fee-ledger), owners/campus-admins see all seven. Verified the Admission Controller's inquiry form works (setup `GET /campuses|/classes|/sections` carry no `@Roles` → open to any authenticated user). **Live-verified** on the real demo accounts: accountant reaches `/reports/*` + `/fees/invoices` (200) and is denied `/vacancies` (403); admission controller loads. *(Reset the demo `acc.girls@demo.pk` + `admissions-girls@demo.pk` passwords to `Test!Secret12` for the live pass.)*

## ➕ Business rule — one campus admin per campus (2026-07-21)
- [x] **A campus may have at most one `CAMPUS_ADMIN` (the principal).** Enforced in `UsersService` across all three assignment paths — `create` (`POST /users`), `update` (`PATCH /users/:id` role/campus change), and the grant (`PATCH /users/:id/campus-admin`) — via a shared `assertNoOtherCampusAdmin(campusId, exceptUserId?)` that 409s (`CONFLICT`) when another active (`deletedAt: null`) campus admin already holds that campus. Per-campus, not per-school: a different campus can still have its own principal; re-saving the existing principal is not a false conflict (`exceptUserId`). Covered by `users.e2e` ("allows at most one campus admin per campus": 2nd on campus A → 409, campus B → 201, grant to a campus-A employee → 409). The Campus Hub / recruitment access-manager UIs already surface the 409 as an error toast. *(Optional future hardening: a partial unique index `users(campus_id) WHERE 'CAMPUS_ADMIN' = ANY(roles) AND deleted_at IS NULL` for race-proofing — the service check suffices for this low-concurrency owner action.)*

## ➕ Access-grant consolidation — one `/access` toggle (2026-07-21)
- [x] **Collapsed the two role-grant endpoints into one non-conflicting path.** Removed `PATCH /users/:id/hr-access` and `PATCH /users/:id/campus-admin` (+ the `setHrAccess`/`setCampusAdminAccess` service wrappers + `GrantAccessDto`); replaced with a single **`PATCH /users/:id/access { role, grant }`** (`SetAccessDto`, owner-only). One service method `setAccess(id, role, grant)` validates `role ∈ ACCESS_GRANTABLE_ROLES` (`HR_MANAGER | CAMPUS_ADMIN | ACCOUNTANT | ADMISSION_CONTROLLER`), maps the role to its audit-action pair (HR/campus-admin keep their specific actions; the rest use new generic `ROLE_ACCESS_GRANTED/REVOKED`), and delegates to the unchanged `grantRole` core — so all campus rules (campus-required for CAMPUS_ADMIN, the **one-campus-admin-per-campus** 409) and idempotency/audit still apply. **Principle:** access is a capability toggled on an existing employee's account (reuses their login — never mints a new email/password); `POST /users` stays only for genuinely new people. Frontend `api.users.setHrAccess/setCampusAdmin` repointed to `/access` (JS names kept, recruitment "Manage access" panel unchanged) + new generic `api.users.setAccess(id, role, grant)`.
- **Gates:** api+worker build ✅ · web tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · `hr-access` + `users` + `matrix-conformance` e2e green (173) — updated `hr-access.e2e` (teacher now on a 2nd campus so the campus-admin grant doesn't trip the one-per-campus rule) and `users.e2e` to the new endpoint; added a matrix row (`set user access` → owner-only). Frontend Access panel that lets the owner toggle every capability from the Staff directory is the next step.

## ➕ Module (functionality) access — owner-controlled toggles (2026-07-21)
Layered fine-grained, owner-controlled **module access** on top of the role grants: a granted role unlocks its modules by default, and the owner can switch any single module off for one user.
- [x] **Schema:** new `ModuleAccess` tenant model (migration `20260721130000`) — `{ schoolId, userId, moduleKey, allowed }`, unique `(userId, moduleKey)`; **absence of a row = allowed** (grants stay fully enabled until the owner restricts). RLS auto-covered by the 05 loop (coverage check green); FK cascades on user delete.
- [x] **Catalog** (`libs/common/src/authz/modules.ts`, single source of truth): `recruitment.vacancies|applications|hire` (HR_MANAGER), `admissions.inquiries|admit` (ADMISSION_CONTROLLER), `fees.invoicing|payments` (ACCOUNTANT). Helpers `modulesForRoles`, `moduleBelongsToRoles`, `moduleLabel`.
- [x] **Enforcement** (`AccessService.assert(moduleKey)`, `apps/api/src/modules/access`): **in-service** (§22.8 — reads a tenant row under RLS, never a guard), **live** (takes effect immediately, no re-login), **OWNER bypasses**. Wired into recruitment (`create`/`close`), teacher-applications (`create`/`updateStatus`/`hire`), admissions (`createInquiry`/`schedule`/`record`/`reject`/`withdraw`/`admit`), fees (`createBatch`/`pay`). A user with a module switched off → **403** on the gated action; reads stay open.
- [x] **Owner control** (owner-only): `GET /users/:id/modules` (catalog for the user's roles + on/off state) and `PATCH /users/:id/modules { moduleKey, allowed }` (find-then-write, audited `MODULE_ACCESS_CHANGED`; **422** if the module doesn't belong to the user's roles). `AccessModule` exports `AccessService`, imported by HR/Admissions/Fees/Users modules.
- **Gates:** migration + RLS coverage ✅ · api+worker build ✅ · lint ✅ · e2e `module-access` (7: default-on, owner switches vacancies off → HR 403 immediately / reads 200 / re-enable restores / owner never restricted / cross-role 422 / non-owner 403 / audit) + `matrix-conformance` (+3 owner-only rows) + unaffected `recruitment`/`teacher-applications`/`admissions`/`fees` all green (**197 + 8**). Owner UI (a per-user module toggle panel on the Staff directory) is the next frontend step.

## ➕ Vendor console — tenant list pagination + search (2026-07-23)
- [x] **`GET /platform/tenants` is now paginated + searchable** (the flat, unbounded table didn't scale — the demo DB already has 93 tenants). New `ListTenantsQuery extends PaginationQuery` (`libs/common` — page/pageSize≤100/sort) + optional `search` (`@MaxLength(120)`). `PlatformService.listTenants(q)` builds a `Prisma.SchoolWhereInput` (`OR` name/subdomain `contains`, `mode:'insensitive'`), `toSkipTake(q)` + `count(where)` in one `Promise.all`, and returns `Paginated<TenantSummary>` (`{data,total,page,pageSize}`) via `paginate()` — still on the BYPASSRLS platform connection, `orderBy createdAt desc`. Controller: `@Query() q: ListTenantsQuery`.

Provision success resets to page 1 / cleared search.
- **Gates:** api tsc ✅ · web tsc ✅ · web lint ✅ · platform e2e **15/15** (added "filters tenants by search"; updated the two list assertions from array → `.data`). **Live-verified** in the console: page 1→2 (`Showing 26–50 of 93`), `search=otp` → 6 rows / `Page 1 of 1`, Clear restores 93. *(Optional: the ~90 `mx-/imp-/adm-/otp-` debris tenants from earlier e2e runs could be pruned.)*

Added `student:{deletedAt:null}` to `teaching.myClasses` count groupBy (T1 — header count matched roster), `enrollment.list` where (T2 — attendance roster `?status=ACTIVE` fed removed students, markable + SMS-able), and `attendance.markBulk`'s enrollment lookup (T2 write, defense-in-depth). **Verified** on 9th-A (6 ACTIVE, 4 soft-deleted): count 6→2, roster 6→2 (UI), marking a soft-deleted student now returns "Enrollment not ACTIVE". Gates: api tsc, `teaching.e2e` green (`attendance.e2e` 8/9 — the 1 failure is duplicate-SMS from the live worker racing the test, not a regression).

Now a client resolver: reads `/auth/me` → `router.replace(landingPath(roles))` (401→/login). **Verified:** teacher `/`→`/attendance`, owner `/`→`/dashboard`.

Now bases the set on `modulesForRoles(user.roles)` minus denies (owner still gets all). Query read via `window.location.search` in an effect (no `useSearchParams` Suspense boundary needed). **Verified:** chip hrefs carry ids; attendance preselected 9th-A + auto-loaded 2 students; marks preselected "9th — A · Mathematics". ## ➕ Staff actor — My Leaves self-service + leaves self-scoping (2026-07-24)
Live-tested the STAFF actor (provisioned `principal.main@demo.pk` — the only STAFF user with a StaffProfile — as ACTIVE + seeded staff-attendance). **Works:** T4 landing → `/my-attendance`, brand "Staff", My Attendance (data + %), My Payslips (empty state), T5 modules `[]`. **Finding S1:** no leave self-service UI at all — the `staff-leaves` backend (STAFF/TEACHER-permitted) had **no page/nav/api-client**, so staff couldn't apply for/view/cancel leave despite it being scoped self-service. Built it, and closed two backend §22.8 gaps found while wiring it:
- [x] **Backend self-scoping (security).** `staff-leaves` endpoints were admin-shaped: `createStaffLeave` trusted `dto.staffId` (a staff user could file for **anyone**), `listStaffLeaves` had **no self-scoping** (a staff user saw **every** staff member's leaves), and `cancelStaffLeave` had no ownership check. Fixed in `leaves.service` mirroring the parent-scoping already in `listStudentLeaves`: new `selfStaffId()` (resolves caller's StaffProfile, 403 if none) + `resolveStaffId()` — a non-admin (STAFF/TEACHER) is **forced to their own** profile on create (body `staffId` ignored), list, and cancel; an admin still targets any `staffId` (now required for them on create). `CreateStaffLeaveDto.staffId` made optional. **Verified live:** apply w/ bogus `staffId` → resolved to self; a foreign staff's leave is **not** listed to the staff user; owner still sees all (4 across 2 staff).
- [x] **My Leaves page** (`apps/web/app/(app)/my-leaves/page.tsx`, nav STAFF-only, 🗓️): apply form (type CASUAL/SICK/UNPAID/OTHER + from/to/reason, end-before-start guard) + a self-scoped requests table (status badges, `unpaid` flag, rejection reason, **Cancel** on PENDING only). `api.staffLeaves.{mine,apply,cancel}` + `StaffLeave` type. **Verified live:** submit → PENDING row + toast; Cancel → CANCELLED (button gone). *(Nav is STAFF-only per request; TEACHER is also endpoint-permitted and could get the same nav trivially.)*
- **Gates:** api tsc ✅ · web tsc + lint ✅ · `leaves.e2e` **4/4** (admin path unchanged). *Follow-up: no dedicated e2e yet for the non-admin self-scoping (needs a staff-user login in-harness) — behavior is live-verified.* Test artifacts restored (staff account → DISABLED/soft-deleted, seeded leaves+attendance removed). **Not committed.**
- [x] **Architecture audit of the sibling self-service endpoints** (prompted by the leaves hole): `GET /staff-attendance/mine`, `GET /payslips/mine`, and `GET /payslips/:id/pdf` (the last has no `@Roles`) are all **correctly** self-scoped in-service — `payslipPdf` has an explicit `!isAdmin && payslip.staff.userId !== caller.userId → 403 "Not your payslip"`. So `staff-leaves` was the lone divergence from the §22.8 pattern (now fixed). *(Note: STAFF has no dashboard by design — lands on `/my-attendance`.)*
- [x] **S3 — payslip list order (minor correctness).** `payroll.myPayslips()` used `orderBy: { id: 'desc' }` — `Payslip.id` is a random UUID and the model has no `createdAt`, so payslips showed in arbitrary order. Now `orderBy: [{ run: { year: 'desc' } }, { run: { month: 'desc' } }]` (newest pay period first). **Verified:** two runs (Jun/Mar 2026) → API returns `['6/2026','3/2026']`.
- [x] **Attendance % correctness (nit).** My Attendance "% (recent)" counted LATE and HALF_DAY as full-present and ON_LEAVE as absent. Now PRESENT/LATE = 1, HALF_DAY = 0.5, and ON_LEAVE is excluded from the denominator. **Verified:** {ON_LEAVE, ABSENT, HALF_DAY, LATE, PRESENT} → 2.5/4 = **63%** (was 60%).
- [x] **S2 — DONE.** Built 2026-08-01 (`/leaves`, both queues, approve/reject with a required reason); this entry sat stale for six days and was still being read as open on 2026-08-07. Staff-side self-service completed 2026-08-07 — see the increment above.
