## ➕ Student actor — portal review + error-vs-empty fix (2026-07-24)
Detailed live test of the STUDENT actor (real form login `student@demo.pk` → `/me`). **Works:** dashboard (metrics/details/guardians), attendance/results/fees, `/security` (2FA setup). **Strongest isolation of any actor** — the portal takes no `studentId` param; every route resolves the caller's own `Student` via `self()` (by userId). P2 attendance % consistent (63%), T4/T5 hold. Three findings:
- [x] **ST3 — sub-pages swallowed load errors into the empty state (flow bug).** `me/attendance`, `me/results`, `me/fees` used `.catch(() => setRows([]))`, so any 500/network/expired-session failure rendered the normal empty state ("No invoices yet"), indistinguishable from truly having none — a student could believe they owe no fees when the call failed. Fixed all three to the app's standard three-state pattern (separate `err` flag → error branch), matching the dashboard/parent/staff pages. **Verified live:** forced `/portal/results` → 500 shows "Couldn't load your results." (not empty); genuine-empty still shows "No published results yet"; fees with data still render. Gates: web tsc + lint (frontend-only).
- [x] **ST1 + ST2 — portal formatting drift, fixed via a shared helper.** Each portal re-derived status/period formatting, so they diverged: `me/attendance` showed raw `HALF_DAY` + styled only PRESENT/ABSENT; `me/fees` showed numeric `8/2026` + styled only PAID/OVERDUE. Created **`apps/web/lib/format.ts`** — `humanizeStatus` (`HALF_DAY`→"HALF DAY"), `monthYear` ("Aug 2026"), `attendanceBadge` (present-ish=ok, absent=bad, leave/other=warn), `feeBadge` (paid=ok, overdue=bad, partial=warn, pending=neutral) — and pointed **all** portals at it: student `me/attendance` + `me/fees` (fixed), plus parent `attendance`/`fees` and staff `my-attendance` (dedup; attendance is a pure no-op, parent-fees' one delta = a PENDING invoice goes red→neutral, which is more honest). Ends the drift at the source (same principle as backend `attendancePercentFromStatuses`). **Verified live** (student): attendance "HALF DAY" + correct badge colours, no underscores; fees "Aug 2026", PENDING neutral / OVERDUE red. Gates: web tsc + lint. Leftover test attendance on the linked child cleaned. **Not committed.**

## ➕ Admissions redesign — direct admission + student CNIC login (2026-07-25, in progress on `feat/direct-admission-student-login`)
Reworking registration into a single **direct-admission** flow (no inquiry pipeline, no entry test) with the admission controller as the sole admitter, and giving students a **CNIC + registration-no** portal login. **Not committed.**
- [x] **Authorization: student creation = `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER` only** (segregation of duties — *not* owner/campus, by explicit product decision). Locked `POST /students`, `/students/import`, `POST /admissions` to ADMISSION_CONTROLLER; broadened student **reads** to include it; PATCH/DELETE/guardian edits stay OWNER/CAMPUS. Matrix rows added (`create student`, `admit student`) — matrix-conformance 192.
- [x] **Schema:** `Student.cnicHash` (`cnic_hash`, HMAC of the student's CNIC/B-Form) — migration `20260725120000_add_student_cnic_hash`, RLS coverage green.
- [x] **Direct-admission endpoint** (`StudentsService.createStudent`, `POST /students`): DTO gains `campusId` (picker) + `cnic?` + `ageOverride?`; validates campus↔class, **age soft-warn block** (422 `AGE_OUT_OF_RANGE` unless override, audited), reuses `createStudentCore`, and **auto-provisions the student login when a CNIC is given** — `User(STUDENT, ACTIVE, no password, s-<regNo>@student.local)` + `Student.userId` + `cnicHash`, one tx, `STUDENT_ADMITTED` audit. Live-verified (201 + login; age-block; override; no-cnic → no login).
- [x] **Campus model:** a **null-campus `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER` is now school-wide** (`restrictedCampusId`) so it can admit into any campus (the "all campuses" picker); a campus-bound AC stays confined.
- [x] **Tests (strict A):** student creation in 13 e2e specs now routes through a shared `test/integration/support/admission.ts` helper (seeds a school-wide AC + `admit()`); `admissions.e2e` admit runs as the AC. **Full integration suite green — 24 suites / 325 tests** (the lone attendance failure under a running worker is the known duplicate-SMS worker-contention flake; passes 6/6 with the worker stopped). api+worker build + lint clean.
- [x] **#34 — student portal login by reg-no + CNIC.** `StudentAuthController` `POST /portal/auth/login` (`@Public` + `@RateLimit('login')`, tenant-by-host) → `AuthService.studentLogin`: resolves the Student by `registrationNo` (deletedAt null, userId set), constant-time `timingSafeEqual` on `HMAC(cnic)` vs `Student.cnicHash`, reuses the email-login lockout (`registerFailure`/LOCKED self-heal) + `issueSession` (same cookies/refresh). Enumeration-safe generic 401. **Verified live + e2e** (`student-login.e2e`, 3): happy path → 200 STUDENT session that reads `/portal/overview`; wrong CNIC and wrong reg-no → 401 with an identical message; a soft-deleted student can't sign in. `auth.e2e` still green. Security posture: convenience credential, acceptable because the student portal is strictly read-only.
- [x] **#35 — frontend: one-form direct-admission + student portal login.** Two deliverables, both live-verified:
  - **Direct-admission form** (`apps/web/app/(app)/admissions/direct-admission.tsx`, surfaced as a "**+ Admit student**" panel on `/admissions`, gated by the `admissions.admit` module → the AC's landing): **Campus→Class→Section** cascade (class list filtered by campus, section by class, each disabled until its parent is picked), student DOB/gender/full-name + optional **CNIC** (provisions the portal login) + optional roll number, and a **guardian match→link** section — a phone **Find** hits `GET /students/parents/search?phone=`, existing matches default to **LINK** (radio list, keeps siblings under one parent) with a "create new instead" fallback, no match ⇒ **CREATE** (name/phone/cnic/email). An out-of-range age surfaces the **422 `AGE_OUT_OF_RANGE`** message inline with an **"Admit anyway (override age)"** button that resubmits `ageOverride:true`; a CREATE against an existing phone (409) nudges to link. Success card shows Reg No / GR + whether the login was provisioned. A school-wide (null-campus) AC sees all campuses; a campus-bound AC is confined. New api-client methods `api.students.{admit,findParents}` + `DirectAdmissionBody`/`ParentMatch`/`AdmissionResult` types.
  - **Student portal login** (`apps/web/app/student-login/page.tsx`, public top-level route): registration-no + CNIC (no password) → `api.studentPortal.login` → `POST /portal/auth/login` → `api.me()` → `landingPath` (`/me`). Enumeration-safe generic error surfaced as-is.
  - **Verified live** (owner session on the demo tenant): student-login page renders; `/admissions` shows "+ Admit student"; the form renders all sections; the **Campus→Class→Section cascade populates correctly** (Main Campus → 9th → A). Did **not** submit a real admission (avoids polluting the demo DB; the admit/age-block/override/login-provision paths were backend-live-verified in #33/#34). **Gates:** web **tsc ✅ + lint ✅** (frontend-only, no backend change). **Not committed.**

- [x] **Owner self-serve add-student — trialled, then REVERTED (#31 lock restored).** For a testing window `POST /students` `@Roles` was broadened from `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER`-only to `OWNER_ADMIN` + `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER` (plus the matching permission-matrix row) so the owner could admit students directly. **Reverted on 2026-07-25** — both spots are back to **`ADMISSION_CONTROLLER`-only**, so segregation of duties holds as designed. What was kept (real fixes, not part of the deviation): the `/students` page's old `+ Add student` form was **broken** regardless (it 403'd on the AC lock **and** never sent the now-required `campusId`) — it stays replaced by the working **`DirectAdmission`** component (reused from `/admissions`), with the button now gated to **`isAdmissionController`** (was `isOwner` during the trial), so the owner no longer sees an action they'd be 403'd on. **Add teacher** needed no change — `POST /staff` already allows `OWNER_ADMIN` and the `/staff` page's "Add staff member" form (defaults to type TEACHER) covers it. **During the trial** (curl as owner) an empty `POST /students` returned 422 instead of 403 and a full create succeeded (GR 556 / Reg 16, soft-deleted after) — that path is closed again post-revert; the owner now gets **403** on `POST /students`, which is the intended #31 behaviour. **Gates after revert:** api tsc ✅, web tsc ✅. **Still to do:** re-run the matrix-conformance suite against a live API (it asserts the AC-only row) — the earlier browser live-test was blocked by the preview pane dropping the `*.localhost` session cookie (env flake, not the change). **Not committed.**
- [x] **Bug fix — guardian email collision → 500 (now clean 409).** A user admitting a student with a guardian **email** that already belongs to another account (repro: the owner used their own `codebym.ali@gmail.com`) hit an unhandled Prisma **P2002** on `users_school_id_email_key` (guardian CREATE makes a `User(PARENT)` with that email) → leaked as **500 INTERNAL** in the UI ("Internal server error"). `GuardiansService.resolveParent` now **pre-checks** `db.user.findFirst({email})` before the create and throws `CONFLICT` (409) *"A user with this email already exists — leave the guardian email blank or use a different one"* with `field:'email'`. Only a **supplied** email can collide (the no-email path synthesises a random `@invite.local`); duplicate **phone**/**cnic** were already handled. **Verified live** (curl, owner): the exact failing payload now returns **409** with the message (was 500); blank-email + fresh-email paths still 201. Diagnostic test students (GR 558–562) soft-deleted; api rebuilt; backend lint ✅. **Not committed.**

- [x] **Demo-tenant data purge (2026-07-25).** The local `demo` tenant had accumulated load-test + e2e + seed junk: 558 parent_profiles (~530 `Load G*` load-test rows), 16 soft-deleted students, 18 soft-deleted staff, 598 users. Hard-purged everything except the operator's own data — **kept:** student `Muhammad hussnain` (GR 557 / Reg 17) + its `s-17@student.local` portal login, guardian `Muhammad Ali / codebym.ali@gmail.com`, teacher `Tch-001 / ayesha@example.pk`, and the live admin logins (`owner@demo.pk`, `admissions-girls@demo.pk`, `falconschoolgirls*`). **Result:** 1 student / 1 parent / 1 staff / 23 users. Nearly every FK is `RESTRICT`, so the script deletes dependents in order (sms_logs → fee_payments → fee_invoice_items → fee_invoices → discounts/documents/leaves/admissions/enrollments/guardians → students; payslips/salary/attendance/leaves/assignments/timetable → staff_profiles; refresh_tokens → users). Ran as a **rolled-back dry run first**, then committed; `pg_dump -Fc` backup taken beforehand. **Consequence:** the seed logins the e2e suite expects (`parent@demo.pk`, `accountant@demo.pk`, `campusadmin@demo.pk`, seed teachers `TCH-2xx`) are gone — **re-run `pnpm db:seed` before running the e2e suite locally.** Also note ~109 leftover single-use test tenants (`mx-*`, `adm-*`, `otp-*`, `imp-*`) still exist but are empty (0 rows each); left in place.

- [x] **Demo-tenant class structure cleared (2026-07-25).** Operator wanted to build their own classes, so the seeded structure was hard-deleted: **8 classes, 12 sections, 1 subject, 1 fee_structure, 17 fee_invoice_batches, 1 student_enrollment**. All these FKs are `RESTRICT`, so deletion order matters (timetable_slots → teacher_assignments → student_enrollments → exam_definitions → fee_invoice_batches → fee_structures → subjects → sections → classes). **Kept:** the 3 campuses (Main Campus, falcon boys, falcon girls), the academic year, and all people. **Consequence:** the surviving student `Muhammad hussnain` (GR 557) is now **UNENROLLED** — his student/guardian/portal-login rows are intact but he has no class until re-enrolled, so class-scoped screens (attendance, fees, timetable) will show him nowhere. Backup `school-before-classes.dump` taken pre-delete; dry-run first, then committed.

- [x] **Student lifecycle status + owner delete (2026-07-25).** Previously the only signal was `Student.isActive: Boolean`, carrying five different real situations with no reason recorded. Added **`StudentStatus` enum** (`ACTIVE · SUSPENDED · RESTRICTED · STRUCK_OFF · WITHDRAWN · GRADUATED`) + `statusReason`, `statusEffectiveFrom`, `statusEndsOn` (migration `20260725190000_add_student_status`, backfills `is_active=false → STRUCK_OFF`, index `[school_id, status]`). **`isActive` is kept as a derived mirror** written on every status change — ~12 read sites still use it; retire later.
  - **Domain rule (operator-confirmed):** SUSPENDED = *barred from school, keeps portal* (banner "You are suspended", seat held, **fees keep running**); RESTRICTED = the inverse, *still attending, portal blocked*. They are different situations, which is why one boolean could not express either. STRUCK_OFF/GRADUATED release the seat (enrollment → WITHDRAWN/COMPLETED); arrears survive a strike-off.
  - **`PATCH /students/:id/status`** (OWNER_ADMIN + CAMPUS_ADMIN) — separate from the profile PATCH because it is an audited event with a **mandatory reason** (`STUDENT_STATUS_CHANGED`). Server-side **transition guard**: WITHDRAWN is not settable here (it belongs to the §15 withdrawal workflow which issues fee clearance + leaving certificate); WITHDRAWN/GRADUATED are terminal; SUSPENDED requires `endsOn` after `effectiveFrom`. Portal login (`User.status`) is disabled/enabled to match.
  - **Portal enforcement is double:** login already rejects DISABLED users, and `StudentPortalService.self()` re-checks status on **every** read so revoking access takes effect immediately instead of at next login.
  - **Delete:** `DELETE /students/:id` already existed (soft-delete + closes ACTIVE enrollment) but was never exposed in the UI. Narrowed to **OWNER_ADMIN only**, and now **refuses with 409 when the student has any fee payment or issued document** — delete means "this record should never have existed", departures must use status/withdrawal or the accounting trail breaks.
  - **UI:** status pill + status filter on `/students`; row actions View / Change status / Delete (owner-only, red). Change-status dialog shows *what the chosen status does* in plain words before the reason is typed; delete needs the student's name typed to confirm. Student portal `/me` renders the suspension banner. Shared vocabulary in `apps/web/lib/student-status.ts` mirrors the server transition map.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, web tsc ✅, web lint ✅. **`student-status.e2e-spec.ts` — 10/10 green**, asserting: seat held under SUSPENDED, `isActive` mirror, audit row + reason, suspended student still reaches `/portal/overview` and sees their status, **RESTRICTED kills an already-open portal session** (403 on the next read, not just at next login), WITHDRAWN refused with the workflow hint, no-op refused, STRUCK_OFF releases the seat, delete guard. Matrix-conformance green with the two new rows. Note the two error layers: DTO-shape failures are **400** (global ValidationPipe), business-rule failures are **422** (`AppError`) — both covered.
  - **Live UI verified (2026-07-25, owner session, Chrome):** status filter renders all 6; Active pill; dialog offers only legal transitions (**Withdrawn never offered** — it belongs to the §15 workflow); selecting Suspended reveals the end-date field + the consequence sentence; **Save stays disabled** until reason+date (clicked it empty → DB unchanged); saving flips the pill amber and writes `SUSPENDED / is_active=f / reason / both dates` + the audit row; filtering by Active excludes and by Suspended includes; delete dialog's typed-name guard **refused a one-letter-off name**; from SUSPENDED the dropdown correctly offered only Active/Restricted/Struck-off — i.e. the client map and server guard agree at runtime. Restored to ACTIVE afterwards; 2-row audit trail left behind. **Not verified in a browser:** the portal suspension banner (needs a student portal login) — the e2e covers the data reaching it.
  - **`attendance.e2e-spec.ts` duplicate-SMS failure — ROOT-CAUSED, was environmental.** A `start:worker:dev` worker attached to the same Redis consumed the absence job and dispatched it while `drainSms()` dispatched it too → 2 SMS logs. An earlier kill of the worker looked like it disproved this, but the `nest --watch` **parent had respawned the child**; killing the parent tree (`taskkill /T`) made the suite green. **Not a product bug** — the real worker sets the tenant from `job.data.schoolId` correctly. `exams.e2e-spec.ts` flakiness came from the same contention and is now green too. **Full suite: 350/350 integration + 7 isolation + 36 unit, lint clean.**
  - **Test hardening:** `drainSms()` forced *this spec's* `schoolId` onto **every** job in the shared `sms` queue, so any other tenant's job would be re-attributed to this school. Now filters to its own jobs and sets the tenant from each job's own `schoolId` (mirroring `sms.processor.ts`), with a comment warning that a dev worker will race it.
- [x] **SMS dispatch made idempotent (2026-07-25).** A redelivered BullMQ job (retry, or stalled-worker recovery) previously **sent the guardian a second SMS and debited credits twice** — no dedupe existed anywhere in the send path. Migration `20260725200000_sms_dedupe_key`: nullable `SmsLog.dedupe_key` + **unique `[school_id, dedupe_key]`** (Postgres treats NULLs as distinct, so unkeyed sends never collide).
  - **The claim is the QUEUED insert**, written *before* the gateway call, so losing the race means another delivery already sent it → `P2002` → return silently. Ordering matters: claiming after the send would still double-send.
  - **Keyed** (naturally once-per-event): `ABSENCE:{studentId}:{date}`, `FEE_RECEIPT:{invoiceId}:{receiptNo}`, `RESULT_READY:{studentId}:{term}`. **Deliberately unkeyed:** `LEAVE_STATUS` (the job carries only studentId+status, and a student can legitimately get the same status twice — a second request, or APPROVED→REJECTED→APPROVED; suppressing a real notice is worse than a rare duplicate — add a `leaveId` to `SmsJob` if this ever needs claiming) and `MANUAL` (a school may intend to repeat a broadcast). The **insufficient-credits** log is also unkeyed on purpose: that send never happened, so a retry after a top-up must get through.
  - **Covered** by a new attendance case that re-dispatches the same absence job and asserts one log + unchanged credit balance. **Verified non-vacuous** — temporarily removing the dedupe key makes it fail, then restored.
  - **Full green:** integration **351/351** (26 suites), isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, lint clean, api+worker build ✅. **Not committed.**
  - **Deferred:** nightly auto-restore of expired suspensions (worker job), and browser-level verification of the new UI. **Not committed.**

- [x] **Responsive shell — Phase 1 (2026-07-25).** Before this, `globals.css` (107 lines) contained **exactly one** media query: `@media (max-width:720px){ .shell{grid-template-columns:1fr} .sidebar{display:none} }` — the sidebar was hidden **with nothing replacing it**, so below 720px the app had *no navigation at all*. Also `.container{max-width:960px}` was defined but the app shell's `.content` never used it, so every screen stretched to full monitor width (the cause of the "4 columns of narrow boxes" look on the teacher form).
  - **Mobile nav drawer:** sidebar now slides over (`position:fixed` + `transform`) instead of vanishing; `☰` toggle in the topbar, dimmed overlay, closes on navigate (effect on `pathname`), on Esc, and on overlay click. `aria-expanded`/`aria-controls`/`aria-label` wired. Desktop is untouched — CSS owns the breakpoint, React only tracks open/closed.
  - **Three tiers:** ≥1024 sidebar docked at 220px · ≤1024 dock narrows to 190px + tighter padding · ≤720 drawer + single column.
  - **Content width capped** at 1180px and centred.
  - **Tables:** 24 of 28 pages render a `<table>` and only 3 wrapped it for overflow, so a shell-only fix would still have blown out 21 pages. Solved globally with `table{display:block;overflow-x:auto;white-space:nowrap}` at ≤720 — the table becomes its own scroll container, **no per-page edits**.
  - Also at ≤720: `.row`/`.topbar` wrap instead of crushing, `.inline-form`/`.toolbar` go vertical and full-width (overriding inline `minWidth` via `!important` — ~20 hardcoded pixel widths exist in pages), 44px minimum touch targets, and **16px inputs** (below that iOS Safari auto-zooms on focus).
  - **Gates:** web tsc ✅, lint ✅; compiled CSS confirmed served by the dev server. **Live verification of the drawer is still pending** — it sits behind login and the browser tooling had no session. **Not committed.**
- [x] **Teacher form rebuilt — Phases 2–4 (2026-07-25).** `/teachers` was ~28 fields in one flat card, all the same width, with 10 required fields gating a disabled button that wouldn't say what was missing.
  - **Phase 2 — structure.** Split into **3 steps** (① Job & person ② Personal & contact ③ Background) with clickable step pills that tick green as each completes. New **12-column form grid** with intent-based spans (`.f-full/.f-half/.f-third/.f-quarter`) replacing `minmax(190px,1fr)` — an address is no longer the width of a postal code. **Sticky action bar** (`.form-actions`) so submit is always reachable.
  - **Phase 3 — validation.** Required set cut **10 → 6** (campus, position, department, name, email, mobile); everything else is "fill later". This needed the backend too: `TeacherApplicationDetailsDto` had `fatherName/dateOfBirth/gender/cnic/currentAddress/city` **mandatory**, so a partial save would have 422'd — all now `@IsOptional()`. Submit button is **never disabled**: clicking with gaps sets `showAllErrors`, jumps to step 1, scrolls to and focuses the first missing field. Per-field errors appear **on blur**, plus a clickable "N required fields left".
  - **Phase 4 — input quality.** **CNIC auto-masks** to `#####-#######-#` as typed; `inputMode` on numeric/tel fields; `DD/MM/YYYY` hints on dates (browsers render `mm/dd/yyyy` by locale); **draft autosave** to `localStorage` (400ms debounce) with a "Restored your unsaved draft / Start fresh" banner, cleared on successful create.
  - **Photo upload deliberately deferred:** the upload pipeline exists (`POST /uploads` presign → confirm → `fileKey`, JPEG/PNG allowed) but there is **no endpoint to turn a `fileKey` into a viewable URL** — `presignGet` is only reachable via the documents and payslip services. Needs a small backend addition first; the URL field stays for now.
  - **Test contract change:** `teacher-applications.e2e-spec.ts` asserted *"missing details.cnic → 400"*, which my change invalidated. Rewritten to keep the intent (nested validation still bites) by sending a **malformed** cnic, plus a new case asserting a **partial profile is accepted**. **Full suite 352/352**, web tsc ✅, lint ✅, api tsc ✅. **Not committed.**

- [x] **Instant teacher login on create (2026-07-27).** `createStaff` always made the User `INVITED` with `passwordHash: null`, so a newly added teacher **could not sign in at all** until an owner set a password via `POST /users/:id/reset-password` in Campus Hub — a second, undiscoverable step.
  - **`CreateStaffDto.password?`** (`@MinLength(10)`, matching `ResetUserPasswordDto`). Supplied ⇒ argon2 hash + `status: ACTIVE` + `passwordChangedAt`; omitted ⇒ unchanged INVITED behaviour, so nothing regresses for callers that don't pass it (the hire-an-applicant path still creates INVITED).
  - `HrModule` now imports `AuthModule` (which already exported `PasswordService`). Response gained `email` + `loginActive`.
  - **UI:** "Create their login now" checkbox (**on by default**) with a pre-filled generated password and a Generate button. Generator avoids look-alike glyphs (0/O, 1/l/I) because the password gets written down and read aloud; 14 chars, guaranteed upper+digit. After creation a **one-time green panel** shows email + password with select-on-focus, warning it can't be shown again. Unticking the box explains the account can't sign in until a password is set in Campus Hub.
  - **Tested both directions:** with a password → 201, `loginActive: true`, **real `POST /auth/login` returns 200**, user ACTIVE with `passwordChangedAt` set; without → `loginActive: false`, login **401**, user INVITED with `passwordHash` null. **Suite 354/354**, web tsc ✅, lint ✅, api tsc ✅. **Not committed.**
  - **Known gap (unchanged):** the full-profile `/teachers` path still calls `staff.create` without `fullName` or `password`, so teachers added there show as an email in the directory and can't sign in. Same-shaped fix, not yet applied.

- [x] **Subjects in Setup + per-section subject lists (2026-07-27).** Subjects were only creatable on the **Exams & Results** page, so an admin setting up looked in Setup, found classes and sections, and concluded subjects didn't exist (operator hit this twice).
  - **Setup step ③ is now "Classes, sections & subjects".** Each class row shows its **created date** (`Class.createdAt` already existed and was returned — just missing from the web `Klass` type), a labelled **Sections** line and a labelled **Subjects** line, with `+ Section` / `+ Subject` both accepting a comma list (`Maths, Physics, Urdu`). Creating a class **auto-opens that row's subject form** (`justCreated` id round-tripped from `apiPost`) with the toast *"Class added — now add its subjects"*. The Exams page keeps its Subjects card — this is additive, not a move.
  - **Per-section subjects — modelling decision.** Operator asked for each section to be able to study different subjects. **Rejected** moving `Subject.classId → sectionId`: `Subject` is unique on `(classId, name)` and referenced by `exam_results`, `teacher_assignments` and `timetable_slots`, while `ExamDefinition` is per **class** — per-section subjects would duplicate "Maths" once per section and multiply every exam result. **Chosen:** new **`SectionSubject`** link table (migration `20260727120000_section_subjects`) — the class owns the catalogue, each section opts into a subset. That is how electives actually work (9-A Computer, 9-B Biology, shared core). **No backfill needed:** zero rows for a section means "studies everything the class offers", so existing data is unaffected.
  - Composite FKs `(section_id, school_id)` / `(subject_id, school_id)` carry the tenant chain (audit H-3). **RLS is not automatic on migrate** — `prisma migrate deploy` left `relrowsecurity = f`; had to run `pnpm db:sql` (companion `05_rls.sql` auto-covers every `school_id` table) then `pnpm db:check-rls` ✅. Remember this for any future new tenant table.
  - **API:** `CreateSectionDto` gains `subjectIds?` / `copySubjectsFromSectionId?` (copy source must be the same class); new `PUT /sections/:id/subjects` to replace the set; `listSections` now returns `subjectIds[]`. Subjects are validated to belong to the section's class.
  - **UI:** the `+ Section` panel asks *"Which subjects will this section study?"* — **Same as an existing section** (with a picker, previewing the copied names) or **Choose different subjects** (toggle chips). First section of a class skips straight to choosing. No subjects yet ⇒ the question is hidden entirely.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, web tsc ✅, lint ✅ (both), integration **354/354**, isolation 7/7, RLS coverage ✅. `fees.e2e-spec.ts` failed once under full-suite contention and passed alone + on re-run — same flakiness class as `exams`/`attendance`, not a regression. **Not committed. Not browser-verified.**

- [x] **Setup ③ layout fixes + inline subject creation (2026-07-27).**
  - **Bug (self-inflicted):** chip lists used `.row`, which is `justify-content: space-between` — on a wide screen the badges were flung to opposite edges (`Biology ⟷ … ⟷ Translation of Holy Quran` across 1400px, `Section A` marooned right). Added **`.chips`** (`flex-wrap`, `gap`, no space-between) and switched every badge list to it. **Never use `.row` for a chip list.**
  - **Order corrected:** the row now reads **Subjects → Sections**, matching the order the UI forces (a section's subject picker needs subjects to exist first); it previously showed Sections first.
  - **Per-section subjects were invisible** — the row only ever showed the class catalogue, so deliberately giving 8-B a different list looked identical on screen. Section chips now render `Section B · 4 subjects` when a section's set differs from the catalogue, with the names in the tooltip.
  - **Inline subject creation:** in "Choose different subjects" there is now a *"Subject not listed? Add it"* field. Creating there adds the subject to the **class** catalogue and ticks it for the section being created, so an elective the class never offered (8-B takes Biology) doesn't force the operator to cancel out, add it, and start over. Case-insensitive duplicate → selects the existing subject instead of erroring.
  - **Gates:** web tsc ✅, lint ✅. Backend untouched. **Not committed. Not browser-verified.**
- [x] **Edit & delete for classes / sections / subjects (2026-07-27).** These were **create-and-read only** — a mis-typed "Mathmatics" or "9tth" was permanent. Added `PATCH` + `DELETE` for all three (OWNER_ADMIN + CAMPUS_ADMIN, campus-scoped via `assertClassCampus`).
  - **Guards name the blocker**, like the campus fix: a class refuses while it has sections / fee structures / exams / fee batches; a section while it has enrolments / teacher assignments / timetable slots; a subject while exam results / assignments / slots reference it. Deleting a class also removes its (provably unused) subjects; deleting a subject drops its `section_subjects` links first (that FK is RESTRICT); section deletion cascades its links.
  - **⚠️ `teacher_assignments.section_id` / `.subject_id` and `fee_invoice_batches.class_id` have NO foreign keys** — the database will not stop an orphan, so those counts are checked in code. Worth adding real FKs later.
  - **8 permission-matrix rows** added (rename/delete × class/section/subject + set-section-subjects); `MatrixRow.method` widened to include `put`. Matrix conformance **239 assertions green**.
  - **4 new guard tests** in `campus-scope.e2e-spec.ts`: rename a class then refuse to delete it (has sections), refuse to delete a section with an enrolled student, rename + delete an unused subject, and delete an empty section then its now-empty class.
  - **UI:** class rows get inline **Rename** (Enter saves, Esc cancels) and a confirmed **Delete**; subject chips get ✕; section chips get ✎ rename and ✕ delete. All destructive actions confirm first and surface the server's 409 message.

- [x] **DB audit fix #1 — orphan cleanup + missing foreign keys (2026-07-28).** A full database audit found **55 columns that look like FKs with no constraint**, and it had already caused real corruption.
  - **Root cause:** not a migration bug — the Prisma models simply had **no relation field** for those columns (e.g. `StudentEnrollment` declared `academicYear` and `section` but `campusId`/`classId` were bare `String`s), so no FK was ever generated. Fixing it in raw SQL alone would have left permanent `prisma migrate` drift; the schema had to change.
  - **Orphans cleaned first** (backup `before-fk.dump`): **19 `report_cards`** in `demo` pointing at enrolments removed by earlier purges — `enrollment_id` is `NOT NULL`, so they could only be deleted; **5 `sms_logs`** in dead test tenants — `student_id` **is** nullable, so the pointer was nulled and the delivery/billing record kept. Dry-run first, verified 0 orphans and all 5 logs retained.
  - **11 FKs added** (migration `20260728120000_add_missing_foreign_keys`), all composite `(child_id, school_id) → parent(id, school_id)` so the DB enforces the tenant chain: `student_enrollments.campus_id/.class_id`, `teacher_assignments.academic_year_id/.section_id/.subject_id`, `fee_structures.campus_id/.academic_year_id`, `fee_invoice_batches.class_id/.academic_year_id`, `holidays.campus_id`, `inquiries.desired_class_id`. FK count **84 → 95**; remaining FK drift **0**.
  - **⚠️ The migration was hand-written, NOT `prisma migrate diff` output.** That diff also wanted to `DROP INDEX students_full_name_trgm` — owned by the `04_trigram.sql` companion, which Prisma cannot see — and to drop a column default. Taking it wholesale would have silently killed trigram name search. **Always diff-then-curate when SQL companions exist.**
  - Companions re-applied + `db:check-rls` ✅ (RLS is not automatic on migrate). Trigram index verified still present.
  - **Verified clean:** 0 orphans of any kind, 0 duplicate `section_subjects`, 0 duplicate `(school_id, email)`.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **393/393**, isolation 7/7. `fees.e2e-spec.ts` flaked once under contention, passed alone and on re-run (known flake).
  - **Deferred from the audit:** actor columns (`*_by_id`) left unconstrained — nulling an actor would damage audit trails, needs a separate decision. Audit rows on delete paths (#2), partial uniques for soft-delete (#3), 24 unindexed FKs (#4), CNIC-in-JSON encryption (#5), 194 dead tenants (#6), CHECK constraints (#7) all still open.

- [x] **DB audit fix #2 — audit trail on destructive paths (2026-07-28).** **8 destructive service methods wrote no audit row**, and **11 declared actions were never emitted** — including `STUDENT_DELETED`, so removing a child's record left *no trace of who or when*. For a system holding minors' data that is the most serious gap the audit found.
  - **Design point that shaped every entry:** `audit_logs` has **no FK to the entity** (deliberate — the row must outlive what it describes), so after a delete `entityId` dangles. Every new entry therefore records **identity in `oldValue`** (name, GR number, employee code, phone) — an id alone proves nothing once the row is gone.
  - **Wired:** `STUDENT_DELETED` (+ fullName/grNumber/registrationNo/status), `STUDENT_UPDATED` (old→new), `GUARDIAN_LINKED`, `GUARDIAN_UNLINKED` (+ the parent's name/phone, since the link row is destroyed), `PRIMARY_GUARDIAN_CHANGED`, and **5 new actions**: `CAMPUS_DELETED`, `CLASS_DELETED` (+ the subject names removed with it), `SECTION_DELETED`, `SUBJECT_DELETED`, `TEACHER_ASSIGNMENT_REMOVED`.
  - `deleteAssignment` uses `deleteMany` with a campus-scope filter, which is a **no-op when the row is out of scope** — it now reads first and only audits when `count > 0`, so a blocked attempt isn't logged as a deletion.
  - **Declared-but-unwritten: 11 → 7**, and every remaining one is a **feature that does not exist yet** (`MFA_RESET` — no endpoint; `SCHOOL_PROVISIONED` — provisioning has no tenant actor and `record()` throws without one; `FINE_WAIVED`, `GRADE_CHANGED_POST_PUBLISH`, `PII_ANONYMIZED`, `DATA_EXPORTED`, `SUPPORT_SESSION_STARTED`). Correctly deferred, not overlooked.
  - **Renames deliberately NOT audited** — low-risk, and they would bury the destructive entries in noise.
  - **Tests:** a structural-delete case asserting all three actions fire with the **names preserved** and an actor recorded, plus a student-delete case asserting `fullName`/`grNumber` survive in `oldValue`. The first draft failed by finding **6 rows instead of 3** — earlier delete tests in the same spec now audit too, which was the feature working; the query was scoped to the ids under test.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **394/394**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36. `exams.e2e-spec.ts` flaked once under contention, passed alone and on re-run (known flake). **Not committed.**

- [x] **DB audit fix #3 — partial uniques for soft-deleted rows (2026-07-28).** `users(school_id,email)`, `students(school_id,gr_number)` and `students(school_id,registration_no)` were **plain UNIQUEs spanning soft-deleted rows**, so a departed teacher owned their address for ever and a mis-keyed admission owned its GR number for ever. Root cause of the *"Someone already uses the email…"* wall hit earlier, with no visible record to clear.
  - **Domain reasoning for GR numbers:** freeing them is only correct because **delete means "this record should never have existed"** — `softDelete` refuses once fees or certificates exist. A **WITHDRAWN or STRUCK_OFF student is not deleted** and keeps their GR for ever, which is what a school register requires. Only mistakes free their identifier.
  - Migration `20260728140000_soft_delete_partial_uniques`. **Partial indexes are CREATED BEFORE the plain ones are dropped**, so uniqueness never lapses even between statements; mirrored in `02_partial_uniques.sql` (the project's home for what Prisma can't express) with `IF NOT EXISTS`, so whichever runs second is a no-op. Schema keeps `@@unique([id, schoolId])` — that composite backs dozens of FKs and must stay total.
  - **Hand-written again**: `prisma migrate diff` wanted to `DROP INDEX students_full_name_trgm` for the third time. Verified afterwards that the trigram index survives.
  - **⚠️ The DB fix alone was not enough.** Two application pre-checks were *stricter than the constraint* and still blocked reuse: `staff.createStaff` and `users.create` both searched **all** users for the email. Scoped both to `deletedAt: null`. Lesson: when relaxing a constraint, grep for the code that duplicates it — the friendly-error pre-checks added earlier silently re-imposed the old rule. The now-unreachable "taken-by-removed" email branch was removed; the equivalent message for **employeeCode stays**, because `staff_profiles` has no `deleted_at` and a removed staff member's code genuinely remains taken.
  - **Test:** create a teacher → same email 409s while live → remove the account → **re-hire with the same address succeeds**, and exactly one row is live (two rows total, one soft-deleted). Verified 0 duplicate live emails / GR numbers / registration numbers tenant-wide.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **395/395**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, RLS coverage ✅. **Not committed.**

- [x] **DB audit fix #4 — index every foreign key (2026-07-28).** Postgres does **not** index a referencing column automatically, so each parent `DELETE` sequentially scanned the child to enforce the constraint, and every join on that column scanned too. **38 FKs were unindexed**, including the ones this codebase joins on constantly: `fee_payments.invoice_id`, `exam_results.enrollment_id`, `student_enrollments.student_id/section_id/class_id/campus_id`, `student_guardians.student_id`.
  - Added as **`@@index` in `schema.prisma`** (26 models) rather than raw SQL, so there is no migrate drift. Column order matches the FK `(child_id, school_id)`, so the index satisfies the **constraint check** itself, not merely the join. Migration `20260728160000_index_foreign_keys`.
  - **2 left unindexed deliberately:** `subjects.school_id` and `terms.school_id` — the tenant-root School relation. Schools are never deleted in the product (no `DELETE /schools`), so those indexes would cost writes and buy nothing. Indexing `school_id` alone on every tenant table is waste; real queries filter `school_id` **plus** a business predicate, which the composites already cover.
  - **Cost measured:** 174 → **212 indexes**, total index size **4.7 MB**. Acceptable for a read/join-heavy school workload with low write volume.
  - **`prisma migrate diff` wanted to `DROP INDEX students_full_name_trgm` for the FOURTH time** — the trigram companion is invisible to Prisma, so *every* schema migration in this repo must be curated, not taken raw. Verified present afterwards. Consider adding a CI guard that fails if a generated migration contains that DROP.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **395/395**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, RLS ✅, **0 index/FK drift**. **Not committed.**

- [x] **DB audit fix #5 — teacher CNIC encrypted at rest (2026-07-28).** The CNIC (Pakistani national ID) was stored **in plaintext inside the `teacher_applications.details` JSON**, while the *same data class* on `ParentProfile` has been AES-256-GCM encrypted all along (`cnic_enc`). Same sensitivity, opposite treatment.
  - **Own column, not encryption-inside-JSON:** a blob cannot be selectively protected, and a column makes what is protected visible in the schema instead of buried in a document. Mirrors `parent_profiles.cnic_enc` / `staff_profiles.bank_account_enc`. Migration `20260728180000_teacher_application_cnic_enc`.
  - **Write-only, matching the established policy** — nothing in the codebase decrypts a CNIC (only the MFA secret is decrypted, because TOTP verification requires it). Reads therefore expose **`hasCnic: boolean`**, not the value: silence would read as "never captured", which is a different and misleading fact.
  - The service **extracts `cnic` from `dto.details`** and persists the JSON without it, so the client contract is unchanged — the form still sends it in `details` and **no frontend change was required for the write path**.
  - **No backfill:** `teacher_applications` was empty (verified). Had rows existed this would have needed an encrypt-and-strip data step before the column went live.
  - **A pre-existing test failed and was RIGHT to** — it asserted `details.cnic` comes back, the exact behaviour removed. Updated to assert `cnic` is absent and `hasCnic` is true, rather than deleted. Frontend detail view now shows *"On file (encrypted)"* instead of rendering blank.
  - **Scope:** DOB, father's name and addresses stay plaintext — CNIC is the national ID the audit flagged for inconsistency; encrypting the whole blob would break display and search. Worth revisiting as a per-tenant-key change (§32 notes per-tenant subkeys via HKDF are still a follow-up).
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, web tsc ✅, lint ✅ (both), integration **396/396**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36. DB scan: **0 plaintext CNIC patterns** in `details`. **Not committed.**

- [x] **DB audit fix #6 — dead test tenants purged AND the leak stopped (2026-07-28).** The audit counted 194 dead schools; by the time it was fixed it was **280 (279 dead)**, because every suite run added more — measured directly: **one spec leaked exactly one tenant**. Residue: 1113 `sms_templates`, 252 `sms_credit_ledger`, 147 `audit_logs`.
  - **Root cause:** all 26 specs carried their **own hand-maintained `const tables = [...]`** cleanup list, and several omitted `smsTemplate` (provisioning seeds templates per school) — so the final `school` delete hit a RESTRICT FK and failed, and the `.catch(() => undefined)` wrapped around it **swallowed the failure silently**. Hand-maintained lists + silent catch = guaranteed leak. Two of the offending specs were ones written earlier in this session.
  - **Fix — `test/integration/support/tenant.ts` → `destroyTenant()`.** Derives the delete order from the **live FK graph** (Kahn topological sort, children before parents), so a table added tomorrow is handled without touching 26 specs, and **throws if the school survives** — a silent cleanup failure is what created the mess. All 26 specs converted; only `health.e2e-spec.ts` is exempt (provisions nothing).
  - **Verified by measurement, not assertion:** full suite before → after, **delta 0** (was +1 per spec). Schools now **1** — only `demo`.
  - **Purge** used a pass-based `DO` block that retries FK-blocked tables until the graph clears (12-pass cap), dry-run first: 279 deleted, `demo` untouched.
  - **⚠️ Data note (not caused by this work):** demo has **0 students / 0 parents**. Traced through the backups — `before-recruit.dump` (25 Jul 21:41) and `before-wipe.dump` (25 Jul 22:45) both contain the student; `before-fk.dump` (28 Jul 17:17) does not. The loss happened **between sessions**, in a window with no demo-tenant work from this session (the parallel session's commits fall there). `before-wipe.dump` still holds the record if it needs restoring. Demo's `sms_templates` were already 0 pre-purge as well.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **396/396**, isolation 7/7. `exams.e2e-spec.ts` flaked once under contention, passed alone and on re-run. **Not committed.**

- [x] **DB audit fix #7 — CHECK constraints (2026-07-28).** Only **5** existed for the whole domain; money signs, capacity, percentages and date ordering lived **only in service code**, so anything writing outside those services could corrupt silently. Now **21** (`prisma/sql/03_checks.sql`, drop-then-add for idempotency).
  - **Money:** `fee_structures.amount >= 0`, `fee_invoices.total/paid >= 0`, **`fee_payments.amount_paid > 0`** (a reversal is a `payment_reversals` row, never a zero/negative payment), `salary_structures.basic >= 0`, late-fee policy sanity.
  - **Percentages:** exam weightage and `report_cards.overall_percent` in 0..100; `discounts.value >= 0` **and ≤ 100 only when `type = 'PERCENT'`** — a FIXED discount is an amount, not a percentage.
  - **Structure:** `sections.capacity > 0`, `classes.order >= 0`, class age band ordered.
  - **Dates:** academic year end > start, `staff.left_at >= joined_at`, `enrollment.ended_at >= started_at`, and the suspension window `status_ends_on > status_effective_from` — mirroring checks the services already make so they cannot be bypassed.
  - **⚠️ One candidate was a trap.** `sms_logs.segments > 0` looked safe (0 violating rows) but would have **broken `SmsService.logUnverified()`**, which deliberately writes `segments: 0` to record a withheld message without charging credits. The row count was clean only because no withheld log existed yet. Constraint is `>= 0`. **Check candidate invariants against the CODE, not just current data.**
  - **Proven, not assumed:** a transactional probe confirmed capacity 0, a reversed academic year and an inverted age band are all rejected, and a second probe confirmed `segments = 0` is still accepted while `-1` is rejected — the exact intended boundary.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **396/396**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, RLS ✅. Schools still **1** (no leak regression). **Not committed.**
- [x] **DB audit fix #8 — actor columns get real foreign keys (2026-07-29).** Closes the last open audit item. **The question was posed wrongly.** It was recorded as a policy call — *"adding an FK means either blocking user deletion or nulling the actor"* — but that premise is false: **users are never hard-deleted.** `users.service.ts` soft-deletes (`deletedAt` + `status DISABLED`), so `ON DELETE RESTRICT` never fires in the product. There was no trade-off to weigh, only an inconsistency to close.
  - **It was never 0-of-20 — it was 15-of-20.** `fee_payments.collected_by_id`, `discounts.approved_by_id`, `attendance_records.marked_by_id`, `vacancies.created_by_id` and `teacher_applications.created_by_id` already had composite FKs. The defect was that the other 15 did not, so identical columns had opposite guarantees. **`audit_logs.user_id` — the most important actor of all — was among the unconstrained.**
  - **Composite `(actor_id, school_id) → users(id, school_id)`**, so the FK also enforces the tenant chain (audit H-3): an actor from another school is now rejected by the database, not merely by service code. 15 supporting indexes added; `audit_logs` already had a covering `(school_id, user_id)`.
  - **⚠️ The pre-flight orphan check was worthless the first time, and the migration caught it.** It ran as `app_user`, which is subject to **RLS FORCE** — with no tenant context set, every tenant table returns **0 rows**, so "0 orphans across all 16 columns" was measuring nothing. The migration then failed on a real violation. Re-run as superuser: **11 orphaned `audit_logs` rows**. **Verify data invariants as a superuser; under RLS an empty result means "you cannot see it", not "it is not there".**
  - **Those 11 orphans are the evidence for the fix, not an obstacle to it.** All in `demo`, 19–25 Jul, actors absent from *every* school: `ROLE_CHANGED`, `VACANCY_CREATED`, 4× `STUDENT_ADMITTED`. They are residue of the manual demo purges, which hard-deleted users and silently orphaned the trail behind them — the exact damage the constraint prevents, already realised in our own data.
  - **`audit_logs` FK added `NOT VALID`, everything else validated.** Deleting audit rows so a constraint can pass would destroy the history being protected. `NOT VALID` enforces every future INSERT/UPDATE and only skips the backfill scan; `VALIDATE CONSTRAINT` promotes it later with no rewrite. The unvalidated state is visible in `pg_constraint`, so it cannot be quietly forgotten.
  - **`prisma migrate diff` tried to `DROP INDEX students_full_name_trgm` for the FIFTH time.** Migration hand-curated as always. It also emitted an unrelated `section_subjects.id DROP DEFAULT` (benign pre-existing drift) — deliberately **not** bundled into an actor-FK migration. Both remain the *only* drift after applying.
  - **`destroyTenant` needed no changes** — its Kahn sort over the live FK graph absorbed 16 new edges automatically. Exactly the payoff fix #6 was built for.
  - **Gates:** integration **387/387**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, api tsc ✅, lint ✅, RLS coverage ✅, trigram index verified present, 0 new drift.

- [x] **Login lockout from the soft-delete partial unique — found and fixed (2026-07-29).** Audit fix #3 made `(school_id, email)` unique **only among live rows**, so a removed account and a live one may share an address (re-hiring — the intended case). Fix #3 updated the two *creation* pre-checks but **not the lookup paths**, which are the more dangerous half.
  - **`auth.login` did `findFirst({ where: { email } })` with no `deletedAt: null` and no ordering.** With two rows sharing an address Postgres may return the **removed** one, whereupon the `deletedAt` guard rejects a valid password. **Re-hiring someone silently locked them out.** `forgotPassword` had the same defect and would mint a reset token against the dead account — the user resets a disabled account and still cannot sign in.
  - **Reproduced before fixing, and it was deterministic, not flaky:** the existing "frees a removed teacher's email for reuse" test builds exactly that two-row state, so a login assertion appended to it failed **401 vs 200** on the current code, then passed after the fix. The regression guard now lives where the state is already constructed.
  - **A third missed pre-check:** `guardians.service.ts` still searched all users *and* its comment cited `users_school_id_email_key`, an index the live DB no longer has — so admitting a student whose guardian's email once belonged to a removed account returned a 409 the admin could not clear. Scoped to live rows.
  - **Lesson, sharper than fix #3's version:** relaxing a constraint means auditing **reads as well as writes**. A widened uniqueness rule turns every `findFirst` by that key into an ambiguous query, and the compiler cannot see it.
  - **Gates:** integration **387/387**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, api tsc ✅, lint ✅.

- [x] **Parent portal removal — Phase 1: frontend (2026-07-28).** Deleted the 7 pages under `app/(app)/parent/**`, the "My Children" nav entry, and `api.parent.*` + its `ParentChild`/`ParentOverview` types.

  - **`api.studentLeaves` KEPT with a "no caller" comment**, per the Phase 0 default. Its only consumer was the parent leaves page, but the server endpoints still admit OWNER_ADMIN/CAMPUS_ADMIN/TEACHER and the dashboard shows a **"Pending leaves"** metric — so it is the client for the admin leave screen that does not exist yet. Deleting it would have removed the obvious building block for a feature the dashboard already advertises. Flagged in-code rather than silently dropped.
  - **Verified on the running server:** `/parent` and `/parent/abc` → **404**; `/me`, `/setup`, `/login` → **200** (student portal untouched). Frontend grep for `'/parent'`, `api.parent`, `ParentChild`, `ParentOverview`, `My Children` → **zero hits**.
  - **Gates:** web tsc ✅, lint ✅ — no dangling imports.
  - **Still open (Phase 2):** the API continues to serve `/api/v1/parent/*` (401 unauthenticated, i.e. routes still registered), plus the `PARENT` gate in `leaves.controller` and the GuardianOfStudent path in `report-cards.service`. **Not committed.**

- [x] **Parent portal removal — Phase 2: backend (2026-07-28).** Deleted `parent-portal.controller.ts` + `parent-portal.service.ts` and deregistered both from `student-portal.module.ts`. **Live: `/api/v1/parent/children` → 404** (was 401), `/portal/overview` and `/student-leaves` still 401 — student portal and staff surfaces untouched.
  - **⚠️ Two "PARENT" references were NOT simple deletions — they are deny-by-default guards.** Both `GET /student-leaves` and `GET /students/:id/report-cards` carry **no `@Roles`**, so any authenticated caller reaches them and the in-service check is the *only* protection. Deleting those branches as "parent code" would have **widened access** — a STUDENT could have read every leave in the school and any student's report cards.
    - `leaves.service`: scoping **kept**, comments rewritten from "a PARENT sees only their children" to "this is what stops a non-admin seeing everything", so a future reader doesn't mistake it for dead parent code.
    - `report-cards.service`: the guardian lookup could never succeed once parents were gone, so it became an **outright deny for non-admins** — same outcome, honestly stated, no misleading dead lookup.
  - `leaves.controller`: `PARENT` removed from the `@Post()` gate — filing a student leave is now staff-only.
  - **Contract + tests updated in the same change** rather than deferred, so the suite is never left red: removed the `parent children` matrix row; **`PARENT` kept in `MATRIX_ROLES`** on purpose — every row's deny-side now asserts a PARENT-bearing session reaches *nothing*, which is a regression guard worth having until the role is retired (scope B).
  - **`ownership.e2e-spec` rewritten, not deleted.** The property under test changed from *"a parent sees only their own child"* to *"a non-admin sees nothing"*, plus a new guard asserting the portal routes **404** — if anyone reinstates the controller, that test fails loudly. 6 parent tests → 5 sharper ones.
  - **Guardian model verified untouched:** `guardians.service.ts` and `phone-verification.service.ts` intact, `primaryGuardian` still resolved in 5 SMS paths.
  - **Gates:** api tsc ✅, lint ✅, integration **387/387**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36. `fees.e2e-spec` failed twice on a **`beforeAll` timeout** (setup slowness, not logic) and passed alone and on re-run. **Not committed.**

- [x] **Parent portal removal — scope B: a guardian is a `ParentProfile` and nothing else (2026-07-29).** Phase 0 recorded this as a follow-up: *"if parents never log in, creating a `User` + placeholder email per guardian is pure waste."* Now done. Admitting a student no longer mints a `User`; `parent_profiles.user_id` is **nullable** (migration `20260729130000_guardian_without_login`).
  - **Safe because the link was already write-only** — `parentProfile.userId` is read **nowhere** in the codebase (verified by grep), and the guardian read path selects only `{ id, fullName, phone }`. Nullable rather than dropped, so legacy rows keep their link and no backfill is needed.
  - **The `email` moved onto the profile rather than being dropped.** It previously lived on the retired `User` row and was **write-only — never displayed anywhere**. But the admission form *and* the CSV import both collect it, so removing the column would have silently discarded operator input. It is contact data: no uniqueness, no auth path reads it.
  - **A whole failure mode vanished with the row.** Guardian emails no longer share a namespace with staff/admin logins, so a real address already held by a live account can no longer 409 the front desk out of admitting a student. The placeholder `p-<uuid>@invite.local` synthesis and its collision pre-check were deleted outright, not patched.
  - **`PARENT` kept in the `Role` enum** — a LOCKED catalog ([[consistency-register]] §31); removing a value is a type rebuild for zero behavioural gain. Reserved and unused for new rows; the matrix still asserts a PARENT-bearing session reaches nothing.
  - **Two new regression guards** in `admissions.e2e-spec`: admitting mints **0** `PARENT` users and **0** `@invite.local` addresses; and a supplied guardian email lands on the profile while creating **no** user account.
  - **Gates:** integration **389/389**, isolation 7/7, unit 36/36, api tsc ✅, lint ✅, RLS ✅. `migrate diff` tried the trigram `DROP INDEX` a **sixth** time — curated out again.

- [x] **Parent portal removal — Phase 5: documentation (2026-07-29). Removal COMPLETE.** The brain's area notes still described the portal as a live feature, which is exactly the drift the golden rule exists to prevent.
  - **Corrected:** [[Product Overview]] daily-users list (parents are **recipients**, not users); [[Exams & Report Cards]] built-banner, the PUBLISHED-visibility rule and the pre-signed-URL sentence (staff/student fetch the PDF; the guardian gets an SMS, not a link); [[Roadmap & Milestones]] M5 exit criterion; [[Testing & Quality]] E2E journey list.
  - **⚠️ [[Enrollment & Admissions]] was wrong twice over.** It promised *"New parent → User(`[PARENT]`, INVITED) + ParentProfile + **SMS set-password link**"*. The `User` half is now obsolete — but **the set-password SMS was never implemented at all**. The account was created `INVITED` with no password and no route to obtain one, so it was unsignable-into from the day the line was written. A note can be stale *and* have been fiction; corrected with the history kept visible rather than quietly overwritten.
  - **[[Glossary]]:** added **Guardian** (contact record, not an account) and **Parent portal** (removed) so the distinction survives future readers.
  - **`docs/` and the blueprint deliberately NOT edited.** `docs/` is frozen ([[Maintenance]]), and the blueprint's own change-control header requires a PR approved by Tech Lead + PM — an agent silently rewriting §5/§23 would violate the process the document defines. The divergence is instead recorded in [[Key Decisions]], and the Glossary flags that "parent" in older text may still imply a login. **The blueprint remains stale on §5/§23 by design; a spec PR is the correct fix.**

- [x] **Admissions pipeline chip labels were invisible (2026-07-29).** Reported from the UI: the Pipeline filters showed only coloured count badges, no status text. Not missing markup — the labels were **white on white**.
  - **Cause:** the global `button` rule sets `color: #fff` for solid brand buttons. `.chip` overrode `background` to `#fff` but never reset `color`, so the label inherited white. The count stayed visible because `.badge` carries its own colour, and `All (…)` stayed visible because `.chip.active` restores the brand background — which is why it read as "text is hidden" rather than "styling is broken".
  - **Verified by computed style on the running app**, not by eye: inactive chip now `rgb(26,31,43)` on `rgb(255,255,255)`; `.chip.active`, `.ghost` and plain buttons unchanged. `plainButton` measuring `#fff` is the proof the global rule was the source.
  - **Invariant worth remembering:** any class applied to a `<button>` that changes `background` must also set `color`. `.ghost` and `.nav-toggle` already did; `.chip` was the only one that did not. Recorded as a comment in `globals.css` beside the rule.

## ➕ Admissions mode + the per-campus admission seat (2026-07-30)
Two connected changes, both driven by the operator: *"we don't run entry tests, we just fill the form and admit"*, and *"only one person can admit students in one campus"*.

- [x] **`admissionsMode` — the enquiry pipeline is now opt-in (§8).** New `SchoolSettings.admissionsMode` (`DIRECT` default | `PIPELINE`), shipped on **`/auth/me`** so the shell picks the right surface with no second fetch and no wrong-page flash. In DIRECT the `/admissions` screen is just the admission form; the funnel, the inquiry table and `+ New inquiry` are gone.
  - **Why hide rather than leave it:** every metric on that page (`open`, `testsToday`, `readyToAdmit`, `admittedThisMonth`, `conversionRate`) is computed from **`Inquiry` rows**, and a direct admission creates none — so for a DIRECT school those tiles read **0 for ever by construction**, not because the school is new. A half-used enquiry register also makes the Director's conversion report fiction, which is worse than no register.
  - **Not deleted:** the pipeline is built, unit-tested (`inquiry-state-machine.spec.ts`) and campus-scoped — a school that runs entry tests still needs it. This is a school-level setting, deliberately **not** the per-user `module_access` (that would need toggling per account, and a new hire would silently get the pipeline back).
- [x] **Owner dead-end fixed.** `/admissions` gated `+ Admit student` on the **module** `admissions.admit`, but `AccessService.enabledModulesForSelf` hands OWNER_ADMIN **every** module key while `POST /students` is `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER`-only — so the owner saw a button guaranteed to 403. Now gated on **role AND module** (module kept so the owner can still switch admitting off for an officer), matching `/students`. Same class of bug as the chip-colour one: the same action guarded two different ways in two places.
- [x] **The admission seat — one officer per campus, campus compulsory (§8/§23).**
  - **Reframing that drove the design:** with one holder per campus, the thing being managed is *the campus's seat*, not *a person's permission*. A per-person toggle cannot show you a campus with **nobody**, and cannot express the everyday action, which is **handover**, not add.
  - **Service:** `assertNoOtherCampusAdmin` generalised to `assertSoleCampusSeat(role, campusId, except)` over `SOLE_CAMPUS_SEAT_ROLES` (CAMPUS_ADMIN + ADMISSION_CONTROLLER), wired on **all three** write paths (`create`, `update`, `grantRole`) — leaving any one unguarded reopens the hole. Campus is now required for both seat roles, not just CAMPUS_ADMIN.
  - **⚠️ The real hole this closes:** `restrictedCampusId` special-cased a campus-less ADMISSION_CONTROLLER as **school-wide** — so granting admissions to an employee with no campus silently handed them *every* campus. The special case is deleted; a campus-less AC now falls through to `NO_CAMPUS` and fails closed.
  - **DB backstop:** two partial unique indexes in `02_partial_uniques.sql` (`users_one_campus_admin_per_campus`, `users_one_admission_controller_per_campus`) — a service check is read-then-write, so two owners assigning at once could both pass it. **Proved with a rolled-back probe:** first insert accepted, second refused by name. Partial on `deleted_at IS NULL` so a departed officer frees the seat instead of locking the campus out for ever. Campus-admin got the same index, retiring the "optional future hardening" note from the 2026-07-21 one-per-campus entry.
  - **API:** `GET /admission-officers` (per-campus holder, campus-scoped), **`PUT /admission-officers/:campusId`** (assign *or* hand over in **one** request), `DELETE` (vacate). Handover is atomic and **removes the outgoing holder's role before adding the new one** — uniqueness is checked per statement, so the reverse order collides on the index even though the end state is legal. Audited as one `ADMISSION_OFFICER_ASSIGNED` row naming **both** parties (two unrelated grant/revoke rows would not prove they were the same decision).
  - **UI:** `/admissions-team` rebuilt as the campus-first assignment desk — one card per campus with the holder, the branded portal link, and Assign / Change person / Remove; the picker offers only that campus's active staff. **Nav opened to OWNER_ADMIN** — it was `CAMPUS_ADMIN`-only, so the one person allowed to assign the seat could not find the screen. The old "create a new login" form is retired (an officer is always an existing employee), and `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER` is **removed from the Staff capability list** so exactly one place in the UI assigns the seat.
- [x] **Test fixture consequence — the shared helper seeded an illegal user.** `test/integration/support/admission.ts` created a **campus-less** AC (the school-wide shape) and **16 specs** used it. It now takes a required `campusId`; `campus-scope.e2e-spec.ts` seeds **two** officers (legal — different campuses), which also makes its cross-campus assertions sharper: neither officer can reach the other's campus, which one school-wide controller previously could.
- **New spec** `admission-officer.e2e-spec.ts` (7): vacant→assigned, second holder refused **409**, campus-less refused **422**, other-campus person refused, handover moves the seat + audits both parties + leaves the outgoing holder's login and other roles intact, an officer **cannot admit into another campus**, vacate frees the seat. **Verified non-vacuous** — neutering `assertSoleCampusSeat` makes exactly the one-per-campus case fail, then restored. 3 matrix rows added.
- **Gates:** api+worker build ✅ · web tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · web lint ✅ · RLS coverage ✅ · **full `pnpm test` 457 green** (unit 36 · integration 414 / 27 suites · isolation 7), no flakes.
- **Live-verified on the real `demo` tenant** (owner session): `/auth/me` returns `admissionsMode: DIRECT`; owner's `/admissions` shows no funnel and no 403-bound button; the seat lifecycle ran end-to-end — assign Ayesha → granting Haris the role **409s with the handover message** → hand over to Haris (response names both) → vacate. **Demo restored to vacant**, both teachers back to `{TEACHER}`, audit trail retained.
- **⚠️ Not browser-verified:** the rebuilt `/admissions-team` screen. The preview pane would not hold a `*.localhost` session this run (the known env flake), so the desk was proven through the API and compile gates, not by clicking it. Worth a browser pass next session.
- **Operational note:** `demo` has **0 admission officers and 0 students**, so nobody there can admit until a seat is assigned (two clicks on the new screen). The old `admissions-girls@demo.pk` was lost in an earlier purge.

## ➕ Guardian optional at admission (2026-07-30)
Operator: *"to admit a student we need to fill this whole form — make the guardian optional."* A walk-in can now be seated before the guardian's details are collected.

- **The codebase was already tolerant — only three things forced it.** Traced every consumer first: SMS dispatch resolves `primaryGuardian` and **returns quietly when there is none** (all 4 types), fee **advance auto-application** bails on a missing `parentId`, the student detail screen already rendered *"None."*, reports never touch guardians, and `student_guardians` is a link table where **zero rows is already valid — no migration**. The one-primary partial unique is `WHERE is_primary = true`, so it is unaffected. Only `CreateStudentDto.guardian!`, `createStudentCore`'s unconditional `resolveParent`, and the form required it.
- **⚠️ The risk is silence, not breakage.** A guardian-less student receives **no absence, fee-receipt or result SMS — ever**, and nothing said so. Schools that make this optional without a safety net accumulate contactless minors and only discover it in an emergency. So the design rule was **optional to enter, never invisible**:
  - `GET /students` returns **`hasGuardian`** (a `_count`, not the guardian rows — presence shouldn't inflate the directory payload).
  - Directory rows carry a **`no guardian`** badge; a **⚠️ Missing guardian** chip filters to exactly those students (`?missingGuardian=true` → `guardians: { none: {} }`). That chase list is what stops "record it later" meaning "never".
  - The form states the consequence **before** submit, not after.
- **Half a guardian is still rejected.** The DTO keeps `@ValidateNested` under `@IsOptional`, and the form only omits the object when the section is **completely untouched** (`guardianTouched`) — so a partly-filled guardian 400s instead of being silently dropped. Omitting is a deliberate choice; a typo is not.
- **`POST /students/:id/guardians` opened to `ADMISSION_CONTROLLER`** (add only — edit/remove stay OWNER/CAMPUS). Without this, deferring is a dead end: the officer who admitted the student could not complete the record, only an owner could. Matrix row added.
- **`CreatedStudent.parentId` is now `string | null`** — callers already tolerated it; the direct-admission and admissions-admit paths still pass a guardian when the form supplies one.
- **Deliberately unchanged: CSV import still requires `guardianName`/`guardianPhone`.** A bulk file of 200 contactless students is the disaster case this feature's safety net exists to prevent, and an import is a reviewable, deliberate act unlike a walk-in. Flagged to the operator rather than changed unilaterally.
- **New spec** `guardian-optional.e2e-spec.ts` (4): admit with no guardian at all (asserts **0** links **and 0 ParentProfiles** — no placeholder parent invented), the flag + chase list are correct across a with/without pair, a half-filled guardian **400s**, and the admission officer can close the gap later with the late guardian becoming **primary** (otherwise SMS would still reach nobody).
- **Gates:** api+worker build ✅ · web tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · web lint ✅ · **full `pnpm test` 467 green** (unit 36 · integration 424 / 28 suites · isolation 7).
- **✅ Browser-verified end-to-end (2026-07-30)** on the real `demo` tenant. Rather than reset the operator's officer password, a **temporary** officer was created, given the seat, used for the test, then removed and the seat handed back — all through the new endpoints, which incidentally exercised handover twice more live. Confirmed as the officer: landing straight on the admit form, the **"Guardian — optional"** heading with its no-SMS warning, **Admit student enabled with every guardian field blank**, and a successful admission (**Reg No 23 / GR 563**, "no portal login" notice since no CNIC). Confirmed as owner: **🎓 Admission Portal** now in the sidebar, the student listed with a **`no guardian`** badge, and the **⚠️ Missing guardian** chip filtering to exactly that one row with its explanatory note. **Demo restored**: test student deleted, temp officer removed, seat back with `ayeshakhan@gmail.com`, 0 live students.
- **🐛 Found and fixed during that pass — a dead-end I had introduced.** The DIRECT-mode "View all students →" link 403'd for an admission officer: `GET /students` admits the role, but the Students *page* is nav-gated to OWNER/CAMPUS_ADMIN, so the officer landed on "Not authorized". Now wrapped in the existing `canReach(roles, href)` helper (both the header link and the non-admitter card). **Exactly the bug class fixed earlier the same day** — an action offered to someone who cannot complete it — which is why `canReach` exists; I simply failed to use it on a link I added.
- **Tooling note:** the preview pane's **screenshot** action times out in this environment (renderer capture is broken) while `read_page` / `get_page_text` / `javascript_tool` work fine. Coordinate-based clicks and scrolls depend on a prior screenshot, so they are unavailable — drive by `ref` and fall back to a DOM `.click()` for controls below the fold. A stale tab also silently swallows input (typing "succeeds" but no request fires): open a fresh tab if interactions stop landing.
- **Not tested: the SMS silence itself.** The `if (!guardian) return` guards are pre-existing and untouched, so no new coverage was added there — worth a case if absence-SMS behaviour for guardian-less students ever needs pinning.

## ➕ Student CNIC readable by an audited reveal + portal-login status on the profile (2026-07-30)
Operator asked for the CNIC on the student profile. It **could not simply be displayed** — only `cnic_hash` (an HMAC login factor) existed, and a digest cannot be reversed. Showing it required changing how it is stored, which reverses audit fix #5's write-only rule, so it was put to the operator as an explicit decision rather than assumed.

- **Decision (operator, 2026-07-30): store encrypted, reveal to admins.** New `students.cnic_enc` (migration `20260730120000`, AES-256-GCM, same `FieldEncryption` as guardian/teacher CNICs). `cnic_hash` **stays** — the two are not interchangeable: the hash answers *"does this match?"* at login, the ciphertext answers *"what is it?"* for board forms and certificates. A school genuinely needs the second; teacher CNICs never did, which is why that one remains write-only.
- **Reveal is its own audited endpoint, not a profile field.** `GET /students/:id/cnic` (OWNER/CAMPUS_ADMIN — deliberately **not** the admission officer, who captures it but has no reason to read it back) writes `STUDENT_CNIC_REVEALED`. Had the value ridden along with the profile load, every page view would decrypt and there would be nothing meaningful to audit. **The audit row records identity (name + GR), never the number** — a trail must not become a second copy of the secret.
- **`getOne` now strips both secret columns** and returns `hasCnic` / `cnicRevealable` / `portalLoginEnabled`. Three CNIC states, because **"never captured" and "captured but unreadable" are different facts**: a CNIC recorded before this migration still verifies a login but cannot be shown, and the UI says exactly that instead of implying it was lost. **No backfill is possible** — a digest cannot be reversed.
- **Portal-login status added** (operator's other pick): the profile now states whether the student can sign in and with which registration number. Previously invisible — there was no way to tell whether a login existed.
- **Gates:** api+worker build ✅ · web tsc ✅ · root lint ✅ · web lint ✅ · **full `pnpm test` 474 green** (unit 36 · integration 431 / 28 suites · isolation 7) · RLS coverage ✅ after `db:sql` (**again not automatic on migrate**).
- **✅ Browser-verified on the real demo tenant.** Temp officer admitted a student **with** a CNIC (Reg 39 / GR 579, "portal login provisioned"); as owner the profile showed **`•••••-•••••••-•` + Reveal**, the reveal returned **exactly `42101-7654321-3`** with a Hide toggle, and the pre-existing student Abdullah Mohsin correctly showed the legacy state *"on file — recorded before it could be shown"*. Confirmed in the DB: audit row written with actor + student identity, **0** audit rows containing the raw CNIC, **0** plaintext CNIC on the student row, and the **student portal login still returns 200** with that CNIC (reveal does not disturb the login factor). Demo restored: both test students deleted, temp officer removed, seat back with `ayeshakhan@gmail.com`; the operator's own 15 students untouched.
- **⚠️ Consequence for existing data:** all 15 live demo students are `cnic_hash`-only, so **none of their CNICs can ever be displayed**. Making them revealable requires re-entering the CNIC on an existing student — which is the *same missing capability* as the false "Add a CNIC later to enable it" message on the admission success card (`UpdateStudentDto` carries only fullName/gender/dateOfBirth, so there is **no way to add or change a CNIC after admission**). One endpoint fixes both. Not built — flagged to the operator.

## ➕ DIRECT mode: hide the admissions surface from everyone who cannot admit (2026-07-30)
Operator, seeing an Admissions nav item whose whole page said *"Admissions are handled by the Admission Controller"*: **"remove this admissions item from the owner admin portal."**

- **Not a nav deletion — a mode-aware rule.** Dropping OWNER_ADMIN from the `/admissions` entry would break every **PIPELINE** school, where the owner really does manage inquiries there. Nav gating was role-only; it now takes the school's `admissionsMode` too, via one `isUsable(item, roles, mode)` used by **both** `groupedNav` and `canReach`, so the sidebar and every link-hiding call can never disagree. In DIRECT, `/admissions` is shown to **ADMISSION_CONTROLLER only**.
- **The route stays reachable by URL** — the explanatory card is still the right answer for someone who lands there. This governs what is *advertised*, not what is *permitted*; authorization is untouched.
- **Same leftover on the dashboard.** The owner's *Pipelines* section rendered an Admissions card reading *"0 open inquiries · 0% conversion"* — every figure counts `Inquiry` rows, so in a DIRECT school it can never move. The summary fetch is now skipped entirely in DIRECT mode (a metric that cannot change is worse than no metric), and both `canReach` calls pass the mode.
- **✅ Browser-verified both directions**, which mattered because hiding it from the officer would have removed the only way to admit: **owner** → Admissions gone from the sidebar, Pipelines shows Recruitment only; **admission officer** (temp account) → still has Admissions and lands on the working admit form. Demo restored (seat back with `ayeshakhan@gmail.com`, 0 temp users; the operator's 16 students untouched).
- **Gates:** web tsc ✅ · web lint ✅ (frontend-only; no API change).
- **Note:** the owner's curl session 401'd mid-cleanup (15-minute access token expiry) — re-login and finish, never leave a temporary account holding the admission seat.

## ➕ Classes screen — UX audit and remediation (2026-07-30)
Operator: *"too confusing for a non-technical person."* Audited the page and rated it **5/10** — around 7.5/10 for a power user, **3.5/10 for a school office admin**, its actual user. 16 issues found; the 12 worth fixing are done (frontend-only).

- **Root cause, not a list of nits: the page was organised around the data model, not the job.** Class → Subjects → Sections were three parallel editable lists mirroring the tables, while the user's real questions ("set up my classes", "who teaches 9-A Maths?", "how full is 8-B?") had no first-class path. Second root cause: **every control weighed the same**, so one-off setup actions were louder than daily ones.
- **🔴 The worst offender was a placeholder.** `Class name` used `placeholder="9th"` — which reads as a *filled-in value*, and 9th usually already exists. Users clicked **Add class**, nothing happened (button disabled), and the explanation *"Enter a class name."* sat to the **right of the button** where nobody looks. Now `e.g. 9th`, with the blocker text **under the field it refers to**.
- **Primary action moved to the top.** Add-class was below every class card — the first thing a new school needs was the last thing on the page. Extracted into an `AddClassForm` component with its own busy state and Enter-to-submit.
- **Action hierarchy rebuilt to match frequency of use.** Visible per row: **👩‍🏫 Teachers** (was buried in `⋯`, and is the highest-value action on the page) and **+ Section**. Everything else moved into the menu **as words**: Add subject · Edit name & age · View students · **Move earlier / Move later** · Delete class. That retires the two bare `↑ ↓` icon buttons, and the menu now states *why* order matters — **"Order decides the next class at promotion"** — which was previously undiscoverable.
- **Every number labelled.** `6/40` → **`6 of 40 seats`** (was it students? subjects? seats?). `16/200` → `16 of 200`. **"Distinct subjects" → "Subjects taught"** — "distinct" is a programmer's word for de-duplicated, and the figure (11) contradicted the chips on screen (20) with nothing explaining why; a tooltip now says so. **"Ready" → "Ready to admit"** with a tooltip giving the criteria.
- **Only the exception is stated.** Section chips repeated *"all 7 subjects"* on every row, pushing an advanced concept (elective splits) into everyone's face. Now silent when a section matches its class, and **`own subjects (6 of 7)`** only when it genuinely differs.
